# GigsFill — Complete System Reference

**Purpose of this document.** This is a self-contained reference for the GigsFill codebase. If you're starting a new chat with Claude, paste this whole file in your first message and Claude will have a working understanding of the entire system without re-reading the code.

**Last updated:** August 21, 2026.

## Changelog

The list below tracks meaningful changes after the initial sync from the codebase. Each entry covers what changed in the code AND the doc sections updated to reflect it. Whenever code changes, update the relevant doc sections AND add an entry here.

- **2026-08-21 (Sweep findings: broken daily digest + past-open-gig cleanup):** Two follow-ups from a "look around before you test" pass. **(1) Daily artist digest broken since Aug 18.** [open_gig_digest.py:1619](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py#L1619) had `from backend.services.notification_service import get_platform_timezone` — that helper doesn't exist there (lives in [utils.py:151](backend/utils.py#L151) and takes a SQLAlchemy `db` session), and the scheduler passes a raw sqlite3 cursor. `ImportError` fired every day at digest hour and NO artist got their open-gigs digest email for 3 days (log grep: `send_daily_artist_digest failed: cannot import name 'get_platform_timezone'`). Fixed by reading `platform_timezone` directly from the cursor with an `America/Los_Angeles` fallback. Scheduler restarted; next 6am tick will actually send. **(2) Expired unbooked open gigs auto-prune.** ~44 old open gigs were sitting in the DB with past dates (never booked, never cleaned up) — DB clutter but also risk that public calendars + digest queues surface them forever. New daily scheduler task `_prune_expired_unbooked_gigs` in [scheduler.py `run_scheduled_emails`](backend/scheduler.py) hard-deletes gigs meeting ALL of: `status='open'`, `date < today-2days` (platform-local; 2-day buffer absorbs tz edges), zero slots in booked/pending state, zero transactions of any kind. Uses `delete_gig_completely` from [gig_cleanup.py](backend/services/gig_cleanup.py) so ancillary tables (waitlist, notifications, flyers, contracts, message threads, digest queue rows) go with it. On first tick after deploy the task pruned 44 legacy stale gigs (all from Venue Demo, all zero-txn, safe to remove) and logged `[SCHED] pruned 44 expired unbooked open gig(s) (cutoff 2026-08-20 -2 days)`. From here forward this runs hourly; any real venue-posted gig that never gets booked will vanish 2 days after its date.

- **2026-08-21 (Free Trial mode hardening — 6 fixes to make trial venues indistinguishable from regular except for actual money movement):** Full audit + fix pass on Free Trial (per-venue via `venue_payment_overrides.payments_suspended=1`) so every user-facing surface acknowledges trial mode instead of silently rendering standard "GigsFill will pay you" copy. **(1) Booking + cancellation emails carry a Free Trial banner.** New helper `_venue_on_free_trial(db, venue_id)` in [email_dispatch.py](backend/services/email_dispatch.py) — `send_booking_emails` and `send_cancellation_emails` now inject `is_free_trial` into template vars. Amber banner blocks added to `artist_gig_booked` / `venue_gig_booked` / `artist_gig_cancelled` / `venue_gig_cancelled` via Mustache `{{#is_free_trial}}...{{/is_free_trial}}` conditionals (render engine already supports these — [email_service.py:229](backend/email_service.py#L229)). Artist copy: "GigsFill is not processing payment — {{venue_name}} will pay you directly, no Stripe payout will appear in your account." Venue copy: "Your GigsFill card will not be charged — please arrange payment directly with {{artist_name}}." **(2) Gig modal shows a Free Trial banner BEFORE booking.** [gig_modal.py:874](backend/routes/gig_modal.py#L874) `/api/gigs/{gig_id}/modal-data` payload now includes `is_free_trial`; [gig-modal.js:184](app/static/js/gig-modal.js#L184) renders a prominent yellow banner (`_banner('yellow', '🎟 Free Trial Venue', ...)`) placed right after the BANNED check so it shows above the preferred-status / freq-waiver gates. Role-aware copy for artist vs venue viewer. Also exposed on [`/api/venues/{id}/public`](backend/routes/venues.py) for future consumers (public profile page). **(3) Door-settle emails now honor Free Trial.** [door_settle.py:288](backend/routes/door_settle.py#L288) was a two-way branch (`settled_via_platform ? "processing normally" : "pay the delta off-platform"`). For trial venues neither was right — no payout ever fired, so "delta" copy was misleading (venue owes the FULL amount, not just the door bonus). Rewrote as a three-way branch that checks `payments_suspended` FIRST and emits: "Free Trial venue — please arrange payment of $X directly with the artist, nothing will be charged or transferred." **(4) Free Trial audit rows preserved on cancellation.** [gig_cleanup.py:212 / :260](backend/services/gig_cleanup.py) was `DELETE FROM transactions WHERE gig_id = :gid` — wiped the `free_trial` audit row along with real money rows. Split into `UPDATE ... SET notes = notes || ' | Booking cancelled' WHERE status = 'free_trial'` + `DELETE ... WHERE status != 'free_trial'`. Audit trail of "this booking existed under trial" survives cancellation. **(5) Admin trial-toggle-off cutover uses precise gig-start-time.** [admin.py `_convert_upcoming_free_trial_bookings`](backend/routes/admin.py) was checking `date(g.date) >= date(today_venue_local)` — would retroactively convert same-day gigs that had already STARTED, breaking the trial promise (gig performed under trial, then charged after the fact). Rewrote to compute the gig's naive-venue-local start datetime by combining `gigs.date` + earliest matching-artist slot `start_time` (multi-slot correct) and only convert rows where `gig_start_dt > now_local`. Gigs already begun stay on trial regardless of whether the payout window has fired. Skips are logged. **(6) Retained defense-in-depth guards.** [payout_scheduler.py:220](backend/payout_scheduler.py#L220) still catches the mid-cycle case where a venue flips to trial after a `scheduled` transaction exists. `CHARGED_TRANSACTION_STATUSES` in [gig_cleanup.py:37](backend/services/gig_cleanup.py#L37) still excludes `'free_trial'` so cancellations aren't blocked by the "refund first" gate. Cache-busters: `gig-modal.js ?v=30 → ?v=31` on artist-book-gigs + venue-create-gigs. API restarted; templates re-synced from file to DB via `_populate_email_templates` upsert; all four booked/cancelled templates verified as containing `{{#is_free_trial}}` blocks. Smoke-tested `/api/venues/2/public` (the current trial venue) → `is_free_trial: True` returned correctly.

- **2026-08-15 (Signup email combined + backend email-verification lock):** Two related fixes to the new-signup flow. **(1) Combined welcome + verify into one email.** Previously users got a "Welcome to GigsFill" email and a "Verify your GigsFill email address" email within seconds of signup, and the Welcome CTA linked to /app/user-profile.html — which sits behind the verify-email wall (auth.guard.js:102), so users clicked Complete Profile and got shunted to a verify page, then had to hunt for the second email. The `welcome` template in [email_templates.py](backend/email_templates.py) now includes the verify CTA as the primary action AND role-specific "Once verified as an Artist:" / "Once verified as a Venue:" copy via `{{#is_artist}}` / `{{#is_venue}}` conditional blocks (the render engine at [email_service.py:222](backend/email_service.py#L222) already supports Mustache-style truthy sections). Subject bumped from "Welcome to GigsFill" → "Welcome to GigsFill — verify your email to get started". `/api/signup` in [auth.py](backend/routes/auth.py) now builds the verify token/URL inline (same `_verify_serializer.dumps({...})` + `_get_base_url()` path the standalone verify-email helper uses) and fires ONE combined email in a background thread; the separate `_send_verification_email` call is gone. `_send_verification_email` itself stays intact — still used by change-email + resend-verify flows. Even when the venue entity save partially fails (Audit fix May 2026 part 4), the combined email still sends because the verify link is more important than the copy being contextually pristine. **(2) Backend enforcement of email verification.** The frontend guard at [auth.guard.js:102](app/static/js/auth.guard.js#L102) redirects unverified users to /app/verify-email.html, but that's client-side only — anyone with a valid session cookie could hit backend endpoints directly. New `EmailVerificationMiddleware` in [main.py](backend/main.py) is the real lock: authenticated + unverified users get 403 with `{"detail": "EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED"}` on any state-changing (POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE) API call. Read-only calls (GET/HEAD) bypass. The small set of endpoints the user actually needs while unverified stay open via `EXEMPT_EXACT` (`/api/me`, `/api/me/change-email`, `/api/me/delete`, `/api/me/delete-preview`, `/api/logout`, `/api/verify-email`, `/api/resend-verification`) and `EXEMPT_PREFIX` (`/api/notifications` for polling). Admins bypass entirely — canonical `str(user.is_admin).lower() in ('true','1')` check per CLAUDE.md. Uses raw SQL `SELECT is_admin, COALESCE(email_verified,0) FROM users WHERE id=:uid` because the User ORM model doesn't declare `email_verified` (added at runtime via `_ensure_email_verified_column`, same pattern as [me.py:24](backend/routes/me.py#L24)). Frontend cooperation in [api-globals.js](app/static/js/api-globals.js): new `_bounceIfUnverified(res)` helper detects the 403 EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED response and redirects to `/app/verify-email.html?redirect=...` instead of surfacing an ugly "EMAIL_NOT_VERIFIED" error alert. Added to all four helpers (GET/POST/PUT/DELETE) so any page — most notably user-profile — that catches an unverified action seamlessly bounces to the verify wall. Cache-buster on api-globals.js bumped `?v=11 → ?v=12` on all 7 pages that load it. API restarted cleanly; template file syncs to DB via `_populate_email_templates` upsert on startup.

- **2026-08-13 (Delete Account — 4 more NOT NULL FK blockers):** User (info@backendmusiclibrary.com, id=2) hit `500 Internal Server Error / "Failed to delete account. Please try again."` on `DELETE /api/me/delete`. Journal showed `sqlite3.IntegrityError: FOREIGN KEY constraint failed [SQL: DELETE FROM users WHERE id = ?]`. Swept the schema for every FK to `users.id` and cross-referenced with the existing cascade in [me.py `delete_account`](backend/routes/me.py). Four NOT NULL columns still had no cleanup: `notifications.user_id` (19 rows for this user), `entity_invitations.invited_by_user_id` (1), `email_preferences.user_id` (1), `sms_preferences.user_id` (6). None of these live in the existing "PRESERVE row, NULL the FK" audit-trail policy (unlike transactions / venue_email_history / artist_invitations / reviews), so all four are DELETEs. Fix: appended the four `DELETE` statements to the existing `for _stmt in (...)` cascade with a comment tying them back to this incident. Ran `sudo systemctl restart gigsfill`; user can retry immediately. `affiliate_recommend_emails.sender_user_id` was in the FK list too but has `ON DELETE CASCADE` — SQLite auto-cascades since `PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON` is set at connection setup ([db.py:62](backend/db.py#L62)), so it's not a blocker.

- **2026-08-08 (Deep-audit fix pass — 14 findings from the multi-agent audit, all resolved):** Full-branch cleanup of everything the 8-dimension adversarial audit surfaced. Batches by class:

  **Info-disclosure / IDOR (#1, #11)** — `GET /gigs` ([gigs.py:1309](backend/routes/gigs.py#L1309)) was unauthenticated + unlimited and dumped every gig with venue `notes` and per-slot door-deal economics (`pay`, `door_pct`, `guarantee_cents`) — the hardened `/api/gigs/public` at line 1479 was bypassed entirely. Added `get_current_user` + `@limiter.limit("100/minute")`; both existing frontend callers ([artist.book-gigs.js:804](app/static/js/artist.book-gigs.js#L804), [venue.create-gigs.js:7962](app/static/js/venue.create-gigs.js#L7962)) are authenticated pages so no UI change. `/api/venues/{id}/public` ([venues.py:225](backend/routes/venues.py#L225)) was returning every "Gig Details" field to anonymous callers even though `viewer_is_artist` was computed — added a server-side strip so non-artists lose `has_stage`, `stage_width_ft`, `default_pay_dollars/cents`, `bar_tab_details`, `food_tab_details`, `sound_engineer_details`, `load_in_out_details`, arrival policy, `artist_frequency_days`. Smoke-tested: anonymous now sees only name / address / bio / social / PRO flag / capacity-less lean view.

  **Payments (#2, #3)** — `reinstate_gig_payment` ([stripe_connect.py:1580](backend/routes/stripe_connect.py#L1580)) would charge the venue's card, silently skip `Transfer.create` (multi-slot parent `aid` was NULL from `COALESCE`), and roll back the DB via IntegrityError on the NULL-user_id notification INSERT — leaving the money in Stripe with no audit trail. Fix: refuse up front when `transaction_type='venue_charge' AND parent_transaction_id IS NULL`, and add a NOT NULL guard on `aid` before any charge. Payout scheduler ([payout_scheduler.py:297](backend/payout_scheduler.py#L297)) would double-charge the venue on any transient Stripe error (`APIConnectionError`/`Timeout`/`RateLimitError`) because the attempt-scoped idempotency key + bumped `charge_attempts` guaranteed the next tick would use a NEW key. Split the exception handler: transient network/rate/API errors now `continue` without touching the counter, leaving the row in `'processing'` for the 15-min reaper to reclaim with the SAME idempotency key so Stripe returns the cached success response. Also configured `stripe.max_network_retries=2` + 15s timeout at the top of the scheduler run — the SDK safety net from `main.py:248` doesn't reach the scheduler process (separate systemd unit, never imports main.py).

  **Booking / hold consistency (#4, #12)** — `book_with_contract` ([contracts.py:2632](backend/routes/contracts.py#L2632)) and `book_gig` ([gigs.py:2408](backend/routes/gigs.py#L2408)) lacked the `hold_status IN ('active','exhausted')` guard that `book_slot` has at [gigs.py:5003](backend/routes/gigs.py#L5003). The waitlist_lock only catches "another artist has an unexpired in-flight offer" and misses (a) exhausted holds where the venue hasn't triaged and (b) the race window where the current offer just expired but the rotation scheduler hasn't advanced. Both endpoints now include `hold_status` in the SELECT and raise 403 with a clear message unless a valid `hold_token` is presented. Exempting on token means the currently-offered artist's contract-flow still works.

  **Multi-slot filter blindness (#5, #6, #7, #13)** — Same idiom in 4 unrelated queries: outer `AND g.status IN ('booked',...)` combined with a slot-side JOIN, which drops every partial multi-slot gig because parents stay `g.status='open'` until the last slot books. Fixed in-place: `/api/artists/{id}/calendar.ics` ([gigs.py:8160](backend/routes/gigs.py#L8160)) — moved status filter inside each OR branch via `bindparam(expanding=True)`, added `pending_venue_approval`. `_notify_cancelled_gigs_for_artist` + `_notify_cancelled_gigs_for_venue` ([entity_delete.py:266](backend/services/entity_delete.py#L266), [entity_delete.py:344](backend/services/entity_delete.py#L344)) — dropped the outer `g.status IN (...)` on both; the slot-side JOIN already scopes correctly. Now venues/artists on partial multi-slot gigs actually receive account-deletion cancellation emails instead of silently losing the booking. `process_gig_confirmation` ([scheduler.py:392](backend/scheduler.py#L392)) — dropped outer `g.status='booked'` AND changed the notification-key from shared `'gig_confirmation'` to per-artist `'gig_confirmation_a{aid}'` (via `'gig_confirmation_a' || gs.artist_id` concat), so the dedup guard scopes per-artist. Previously once one artist got the reminder, every subsequent artist on the same gig was silently skipped forever.

  **Digest correctness (#9, #10)** — New table `artist_email_digest_snapshot` ([db.py:1679](backend/db.py) — id, user_id, sent_at, subject, body_html, counts) written by `send_daily_artist_digest` on every successful send. `my_digest_preview` and `my_digest_resend` ([emails.py:341-475](backend/routes/emails.py)) now read from this snapshot so the modal shows exactly what the user received. Fallback to `build_digest_for_user` for pre-fix batches with `stale: true` flag. Preview no longer reconstructs against post-edit gig fields with the wrong (legacy) renderer. `my_digest_history` also switched to snapshot-first grouping so history rows match one email each. **Duplicate-send guard**: `send_daily_artist_digest` now checks the snapshot table for a same-day row before rendering — kills the case where a scheduler restart during digest hour caused `last_email_run=0` to fire the digest a second time. Platform-tz aware.

  **Postgres portability (#8, #14)** — `_setup_conn` ([db.py:347](backend/db.py#L347)) was returning a raw psycopg2 connection on Postgres, bypassing the `_PgCompatConn` wrapper. Setup DDL uses `AUTOINCREMENT` and `?` placeholders, both of which `_sqlite_to_pg` already translates — wrapping the Postgres branch in `_PgCompatConn` unblocks the entire boot path (the dead `_setup_placeholder` helper can be removed later). `_sqlite_to_pg` ([db.py:214](backend/db.py#L214)) — added `strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M', X)` → `to_char((X)::timestamp, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI')` so the minute-bucket used by digest history/preview/resend translates correctly. Both changes are latent today (production runs SQLite) but the advertised Postgres migration path is now actually bootable + digest-page-loadable.

  Deployed: API + scheduler restarted cleanly, all four smoke tests pass (anonymous `/gigs` → 401, `/api/venues/1/public` strips 8 sensitive fields, `/api/gigs/public` still 200, snapshot table exists). No frontend changes required this batch.

  **Post-fix sweep**: after the initial 14 fixes I did a same-class hunt and found 5 more instances of the "outer `g.status IN (...)` drops partial multi-slot" idiom. All fixed in the same commit:
  - [me.py:433](backend/routes/me.py#L433) — venue delete-preview undercounted upcoming gigs. Rewrote to count via `JOIN gig_slots` + DISTINCT.
  - [artists.py:751](backend/routes/artists.py#L751) — artist delete-preview upcoming. Dropped outer status filter.
  - [venues.py:1034](backend/routes/venues.py#L1034) — venue delete-preview upcoming. Rewrote as `EXISTS (slot in active state)`.
  - [gigs.py:2275](backend/routes/gigs.py#L2275), [gigs.py:5214](backend/routes/gigs.py#L5214), [gig_hold.py:437](backend/services/gig_hold.py#L437) — three freq-policy check queries. Dropped outer `g.status IN (...)` since the slot-side status filter already scopes correctly. Without the fix, an artist holding a booked slot on a partial multi-slot gig at a venue wouldn't count against the freq policy — they could book too frequently.
  - **Backward-compat guard on the T-1w reminder** ([scheduler.py:409](backend/scheduler.py#L409)): my earlier fix changed the notification_key from shared `'gig_confirmation'` to per-artist `'gig_confirmation_a{aid}'`, but pre-deploy log rows use the old key. Added an `OR el.notification_key = 'gig_confirmation'` fallback so gigs whose old-style shared row was already logged don't send a duplicate to every artist on the next cron fire.

- **2026-08-07 (Venue Gig Details modal + artist external-gig autocomplete polish):** Three related follow-ups to the just-added Gig Details tab. **(1) Shared "(Venue Gig Details)" click affordance in every gig modal.** New module [venue-gig-details-modal.js](app/static/js/venue-gig-details-modal.js) exposes `window.venueGigDetails.{showModal, mountLinks, isArtist, buildRowsHTML}`. Callers emit an inline placeholder next to the venue name — `<span class="vgd-link-slot" data-vgd-vid data-vgd-name></span>` — and call `mountLinks(scope)` after each render; the module checks `/api/me` once per session and, only for viewers who own or are entity_users of at least one artist, fills each slot with a small italic cyan "(Venue Gig Details)" link. Click opens a top-level modal that fetches `/api/venues/{id}/public` (session-cached per venue) and renders the same flat single-line-per-field layout as the Gig Details tab. Wired into: [gig-modal.js:137](app/static/js/gig-modal.js#L137) (covers artist-book-gigs + venue-create-gigs via the shared renderGigModal) with `mountLinks` called in `mountGigModal`; [venue-profile.html](app/venue-profile.html) inline showGigDetail (Venue row + post-render mount); [artist-profile.html](app/artist-profile.html) inline showGigDetail (same pattern); [public-gigs.js](app/static/js/public-gigs.js) `openGigModal` (same). Script tag added to all four HTML pages. **Refactor**: [venue-profile.html `_renderGigDetailsTab`](app/venue-profile.html) now delegates to `window.venueGigDetails.buildRowsHTML(venueData)` so the tab and modal can't drift apart (both use the same row-builder). Cache-busters: `gig-modal.js?v=28→29`, new `venue-gig-details-modal.js?v=1`. **(2) Artist non-GigsFill gig form venue-name autocomplete consolidation** ([artist-external-gigs.js `_wireVenueAutocomplete`](app/static/js/artist-external-gigs.js)): the suggestion dropdown was rendering each match on TWO lines (venue name + city/state below). Consolidated to one line per match: **bold cyan venue name** followed by muted `(City, ST)` in parentheses, so multiple matches stack cleanly and scan quickly. **(3) State-abbreviation fix on the same modal** ([artist-external-gigs.js:452-457](app/static/js/artist-external-gigs.js)): the `#extGigVenueState` select was populating options with the FULL state name as display text (`<option value="CA">California</option>`) while the rest of the site's edit forms — [venue-edit.html:524](app/venue-edit.html) + [artist-edit.html:647](app/artist-edit.html) — use the 2-letter code (`<option value="CA">CA</option>`). Aligned the artist external-gig modal to the edit-form convention. Left signup-flow state selects alone (they use full names on purpose — new users may not know their code). Cache-buster: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=21→22`. No backend changes in this batch — the `viewer_is_artist` flag added earlier today already carries the auth check.

- **2026-08-07 (Venue public profile — "Gig Details" tab for logged-in artists):** Per user, artists deciding whether to book a venue want the full rig / pay / tabs / load-in picture the venue manages on [venue-edit.html](app/venue-edit.html), not just the calendar + media. That info was intentionally hidden from the public profile back on 2026-07-26 ("Venue Info tab retired — owner-facing details didn't belong on the public profile"), but the goal was "not for anonymous scrapers," not "hide forever" — artist users have a legitimate booking-decision need for it. **Backend**: [venues.py `get_venue_public`](backend/routes/venues.py) switched to `Depends(get_optional_user)` and now computes `viewer_is_artist` via a single-row EXISTS against `artists` (owner or entity_user). The flag is added to the response payload — all the detail fields were already in the endpoint's SELECT (the earlier hide was UI-only), so no schema changes. **Frontend** ([venue-profile.html](app/venue-profile.html)): new `Gig Details` tab button rendered `style="display:none;"` by default and revealed in `loadVenue()` when `venueData.viewer_is_artist` is true; `TAB_NAMES` includes `gigdetails` so `#gigdetails` deep-links resolve. Renderer `_renderGigDetailsTab()` builds a per-section card list mirroring venue-edit's order: **Venue Snapshot** (capacity / default pay with "actual pay varies" caveat / artist frequency), **Stage** (yes/no + dimensions + setup notes), **Sound Equipment** (yes/no + details), **Sound Engineer** (only shown when Sound Equipment=Yes, matching the edit-page conditional reveal so we don't confuse artists with a "No" answer to a question the venue was never asked), **Lighting**, **Bar Tab**, **Food Tab**, **Load In / Out** (arrival time + free-form details), **Public Performance Licensing** (only rendered when PRO-certified, as a green ✓ line). Each section uses a cyan uppercase header + dashed-underline label / value rows to visually distinguish from Calendar/Media tabs. Small caveat line at the top: "Everything the venue has set for artists — visible to logged-in artists so you can decide whether to book." No cache-buster (inline in the HTML page). API restarted; smoke-tested `GET /api/venues/1/public` returns `viewer_is_artist: False` for anonymous callers, all detail fields present as before.

- **2026-08-07 (Hold-Gig add-artist picker — correct freq-waiver copy in series mode):** The venue's post-create Hold Management modal ([hold-mgmt.js:587](app/static/js/hold-mgmt.js#L587)) always showed the single-gig disclaimer "**N artists** under this venue's frequency policy. Adding them will waive the rule for this gig only." — including for holds attached to a **recurring series**. That copy is wrong in series mode: at series-hold-start, [gig_hold.py:85 `_skip_freq_exempt=True`](backend/services/gig_hold.py#L85) intentionally skips the `gigs.frequency_exempt=1` write because the artist's bundled-offer modal already greys out dates that conflict with the freq rule, keeping the policy in force across the series. So the disclaimer was telling venues the rule would be lifted when it wouldn't be. The create-modal picker at [venue.create-gigs.js:492-499](app/static/js/venue.create-gigs.js#L492-L499) already handles this correctly (branches on `recurringCheckbox.checked`), but the post-create management path was missed. **Fix**: `GET /api/gigs/{gig_id}/hold-status` ([gigs.py:9064-9069](backend/routes/gigs.py)) now returns `recurring_group_id` in its payload (was queried but not exposed). hold-mgmt.js branches on `gigData.recurring_group_id`: series mode shows "**N artists** currently under this venue's frequency policy. They can still be invited — their bundled-offer modal will grey out dates that conflict with the rule." (same wording as the create-modal series message), single-gig mode keeps the original "waive for this gig only" copy. Zero backend logic changes — this is a copy-only fix atop existing behavior. Cache-buster: `hold-mgmt.js?v=12 → ?v=13`. API restarted.

- **2026-08-07 (Pre-launch polish pass — delete account FK cleanup, digest preview modal, availability picker/edit + Email Center inline description):** Multi-part cleanup in preparation for launch. **Delete Account bug** — user "Jimmy Demo" (info@backendmusiclibrary.com) couldn't be deleted despite having no artist/venue rows. Root cause: FK NOT NULL constraints on `transactions.from_user_id/to_user_id`, `venue_email_history.user_id`, `artist_invitations.invited_by_user_id`, `gig_message_hides.user_id` were blocking the final `DELETE FROM users`. Fix: those 4 columns dropped to nullable via `PRAGMA writable_schema` surgery on the live DB and mirrored in [db.py](backend/db.py) so fresh schemas match, plus `delete_account` ([me.py](backend/routes/me.py)) now NULLs `from_user_id/to_user_id` on transactions, `user_id` on venue_email_history (both audit tables — retain rows for accounting), NULLs `invited_by_user_id` on artist_invitations, and DELETEs `gig_message_hides` / `gig_templates` / `review_link_tokens` scoped to the user. **user-profile.html Notifications tab** — [user-profile.js:853](app/static/js/user-profile.js#L853) reworded Daily Open-Gig Digest description per user's wording: "One consolidated email per day listing your upcoming booked gigs and open gigs you are eligible to book in the next 4 weeks. Turn off if you prefer not to receive a daily summary." Daily Digest Email History rows are now clickable (whole row, `class="digestMyRow"`) — opens a modal (`_myDigestPreview()`) showing the actual sent HTML in a sandboxed iframe via `srcdoc`. Backend endpoint `GET /api/me/digest-preview?sent_at_minute=...` ([emails.py](backend/routes/emails.py)) reconstructs the queue snapshot for the exact send timestamp and reuses `_render_digest_email` (from [open_gig_digest.py](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py)) — no SMTP call. The Resend button now stops event propagation so it doesn't also open the preview. **My Availability (user-profile) + Member Availability (artist-book-gigs)** — added Edit buttons to blackout rows so users can revise start/end/reason without deleting and re-adding. [user-availability.js](app/static/js/user-availability.js) unified add/edit through a `_uaEditingId` state variable (POST when null, PUT when set) with a "Cancel edit" chrome; [artist-member-availability.js](app/static/js/artist-member-availability.js) gates Edit/Delete buttons on `parseInt(b.user_id, 10) === meId` so only the caller's own rows show controls (backend PUT already 404s on unowned rows for defense-in-depth). **Branded date pickers** — the Block Dates form on user-profile My Availability + the artist Availability tab on artist-book-gigs was using native `<input type="date">` which renders as an unbranded white popup. Swapped for `<input type="text" readonly class="gf-date-input">` wired to `GfDatePicker` instances from [gf-date-picker.js](app/static/js/gf-date-picker.js). **From→To month sync**: picking a Start date now sets `endPicker.viewDate` to the same month (and bumps End's value to at-or-after Start) so the second picker opens where the user just was rather than defaulting to today's month. Same treatment applied to [artist-availability.js:_renderAvailabilityUI + _renderBlackoutList](app/static/js/artist-availability.js) — add form and per-row edit form both use branded pickers; `_isoFromDateEl` helper converts the mm/dd/yyyy display back to ISO for the API. Also mirrored on the Member Availability edit modal in [artist-member-availability.js `_mavEditBlackout`](app/static/js/artist-member-availability.js). **Email Center → Email Notifications inline description** ([venue-create-gigs.html:2544-2646](app/venue-create-gigs.html)): each of the 5 "Fires N weeks/hours before gig date" preferences (Gig Confirmation Reminder + 4w/2w/1w/36h Open-Gig reminders) previously had a stand-alone descriptive `<div>` beneath the toggle row ("Send confirmation email to the booked Artist...", "Reminder that a gig is still open...", etc). Per user, folded each description inline with the "Fires N..." label as an italic cyan parenthetical (`<em style="color: var(--cyan);">(...)</em>`) matching the styling of other in-page annotations (e.g. Waive Frequency Limits' "(Preferred Artists can book even inside their frequency window)"), so the label reads: "Fires 1 week before gig date *(Send confirmation email to the booked Artist reminding them of their upcoming gig.)*". Standalone description divs removed. Cache-busters: `artist-availability.js?v=6`, `user-profile.js?v=18`, `user-availability.js?v=7`. Files chown'd to `www-data:www-data` per the auto-export-breaks-silently convention.

- **2026-08-04 (Demo email simplification + strike-through fix):** Per user: reverted the whole iPhone-Safari-workaround chain and simplified. Most iPhone users don't have Chrome installed, so the Copy-link / Open-in-Chrome fallbacks just moved the problem. **Deleted** `/demo/join/{req_id}` landing route entirely. `_fmt_meeting_line` back to using the raw meeting URL as the primary anchor (no indirection). **Apple note rewritten** to a single friendly recommendation: "iPhone / iPad users: We recommend downloading the **Teams App** before your meeting — it works perfectly this way with no hassles." The **Teams App** text is a styled amber-pill anchor to `https://apps.apple.com/us/app/microsoft-teams/id1113153706`. Kept the Meeting ID + Passcode fallback panel for prospects who already have Teams and might hit auto-launch flakiness. **Trouble line** trimmed: dropped the "we'll switch you to Zoom or Google Meet" tail — just points to `support@gigsfill.com` now. **Strike-through fix** on the "Was scheduled for" red panel in both prospect-side and admin-side reschedule/decline emails ([demo_requests.py:938](backend/routes/demo_requests.py#L938) + line 1035): user reported only the date was crossed out. Root cause: some email clients (Apple Mail / Gmail iOS) inconsistently apply inline `text-decoration:line-through` on divs containing punctuation like the middle-dot separator `·`. Fixed by wrapping the entire prior-slot string in a `<del>` semantic tag with an explicit inline `text-decoration:line-through;text-decoration-thickness:2px` — `<del>` is universally supported across email clients and paints the strike through every glyph in the content. API restarted; landing route now returns 404; resent confirmation.

- **2026-08-04 (Demo landing — iPhone Safari workarounds so prospects can join Teams meetings without installing the Teams app):** Per user's follow-up (with Google support-article context): the "invalid address" isn't a URL bug — it's iOS Safari's cross-site tracking + app-launch block on Teams URLs. Two workarounds that DON'T require the Teams app: (1) copy the URL and paste into Chrome / Firefox / Edge, or (2) open the URL in Chrome (which doesn't apply the same block). Updated the [/demo/join/{req_id}](backend/routes/demo_requests.py) landing page to expose both: **"📋 Copy meeting link"** button using the Clipboard API (with a `prompt()` fallback for older browsers), and **"🌐 Open in Chrome"** anchor using the `googlechromes://` URL scheme (Chrome's iOS registered handler for HTTPS URLs — tap launches Chrome directly). Both live under the primary "Join meeting in browser" button. Apple-device panel rewritten to promote both workarounds explicitly and drop the "install Teams app" pitch. Same rewrite applied to the email's `_fmt_meeting_line` apple note — points iPhone users at the landing page's Copy / Open-in-Chrome buttons rather than telling them to install Teams. API restarted; smoke-tested landing renders all three new buttons + updated messaging; resent confirmation email.

- **2026-08-04 (Demo email — /demo/join/{id} landing page for iOS Safari Teams-link bug):** After the earlier Meeting ID/Passcode fallback the prospect still hit "Safari cannot open the page because the address is invalid" on iPhone — tapping the Teams URL in Mail eventually triggered `msteams://` which Safari rejects. Root fix: the email link no longer points at teams.live.com directly. New route [`GET /demo/join/{req_id}`](backend/routes/demo_requests.py) renders a branded gigsfill.com landing page (iPhone Safari opens this reliably every time) that presents: a big purple→cyan "Join meeting" button, the extracted Meeting ID + Passcode in a copy-friendly monospace block (`user-select:all`), an Apple-device install-Teams hint, and a `support@gigsfill.com` fallback line offering Zoom/Meet as alternatives. `_fmt_meeting_line` now takes optional `req_id` + `site_url` and — for Teams URLs only — rewrites the primary anchor to `{site_url}/demo/join/{req_id}`. Non-Teams URLs (Zoom / Meet work in Safari) keep their raw link. All 4 call sites (`_build_prospect_confirmation_html`, `_build_prospect_reminder_html`, `_build_admin_reminder_html`, and the other reminder builder) already had `req_row` + `site_url` in scope, so the change is a sed-swap: `_fmt_meeting_line(meeting_url)` → `_fmt_meeting_line(meeting_url, req_id=req_row.get('id'), site_url=site_url)`. API restarted; smoke-tested `GET /demo/join/37` renders 200 with join button + Meeting ID + Passcode + Teams URL visible; resent confirmation to prospect.

- **2026-08-04 (Demo email — Teams join fallback: Meeting ID + Passcode + support contact):** Prospect reported "Safari cannot open the page because the address is invalid" tapping the Teams meeting link on iPhone. Root cause is Teams' JS-redirect from teams.live.com → `msteams://` deep link, which fails when the Teams app isn't installed. `_fmt_meeting_line` ([demo_requests.py:330](backend/routes/demo_requests.py#L330)) now — in addition to the existing Apple-device install-Teams warning — extracts the **Meeting ID** + **Passcode** from `teams.live.com/meet/{id}?p={pw}` URLs via a `re.search` and renders them as a copy-friendly manual-join panel ("Can't open the link? Join manually in the Teams app: Meeting ID … · Passcode …"). Adds a **"Having trouble?" fallback line** pointing prospects to `support@gigsfill.com` with an offer to switch to Zoom or Google Meet. Extraction is skipped for the enterprise `/l/meetup-join/…` URL shape (no id/passcode in those). Verified rendering for both URL styles: teams.live.com URL exposes both fields; teams.microsoft.com URL keeps the Apple + support-fallback sections without a misleading manual-join panel. API + scheduler restarted.

- **2026-08-04 (Artist digest email — include external non-GigsFill gigs in Your upcoming gigs):** The daily artist digest ([open_gig_digest.py `_render_digest_email_live`](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py)) has a "Your upcoming gigs" section that lists confirmed/pending bookings in the next 4 weeks — but only from the `gigs` table. Artist-logged external gigs (from `artist_external_gigs`) were invisible. **Added** `_fetch_artist_external_gigs_for_digest(cursor, artist_id, window_days=28)` returning rows shaped like the booked-gig ones but tagged `_is_external: True`, `gig_id: None`, `venue_id: None`. In `build_digest_for_user` the new fetch runs per artist alongside `_fetch_artist_booked_gigs_live`; results bypass `_merge_gig_variants` (no gig_id to key on, no per-artist variants) and get concatenated onto `all_booked` after `_finalize_variant_gigs` with a re-sort by date. **`_fmt_line`** now branches on `_is_external`: date renders as plain text (no /app/artist-book-gigs.html?gig=… link — no real gig id), venue renders as plain text (no /app/venue-profile.html?venue_id=… link — external venue isn't on GigsFill), and the pay segment is omitted from the slot line ("Slot 1 · 7:00 PM – 10:00 PM" without the pay/deal part). Status badge stays green "Booked". API + scheduler restarted; smoke-tested via `build_digest_for_user(cursor, user_id)` — external venue name "14 Cannons" appears in the "Your upcoming gigs" section of the rendered body.

- **2026-08-03 (Show All Gigs — collapsible past section + wired on venue create-gigs):** Two follow-ups after the initial "Show All Gigs" rollout. **Past gigs collapse by default** in both [public-gigs-list-modal.js](app/static/js/public-gigs-list-modal.js) and [artist-booked-gigs-modal.js](app/static/js/artist-booked-gigs-modal.js). The "Past gigs" section divider is now clickable — chevron flips (`▼ Show past gigs (N)` → `▲ Hide past gigs (N)`) and past rows toggle visibility. Bottom summary line shows `X gigs total · N past hidden` when the section is collapsed. State (`showPast: false`) resets on every modal open. Divider only renders when there ARE past gigs. **Fifth wiring**: added the same button to [venue-create-gigs.html](app/venue-create-gigs.html) next to the "Export to Calendar (.ics)" link. Data source is the existing `venueGigsCache` (from `/venues/{id}/gigs`), exposed on `window._vcgGigsCache` + `window._vcgVenueName` from [venue.create-gigs.js:2260](app/static/js/venue.create-gigs.js#L2260) so the launcher script doesn't duplicate the fetch. Same suppressed venue link + artist chip pattern as the public venue profile version. Cache-busters: `public-gigs-list-modal.js?v=2` (all 4 pages), `artist-booked-gigs-modal.js?v=3`, `venue.create-gigs.js?v=242`.

- **2026-08-03 (Show All Gigs list + CSV/PDF export on public calendars):** User asked to bring the artist book-gigs page's "📋 Show All Booked Gigs" button (sortable list + CSV / PDF export) to the three public calendars — artist public profile, city public gigs, and venue public profile. Public versions have no pay column, no Cancel action, and no auth requirement. **New shared module** [public-gigs-list-modal.js](app/static/js/public-gigs-list-modal.js): mirrors [artist-booked-gigs-modal.js](app/static/js/artist-booked-gigs-modal.js) structure (sortable columns, future/past split-sort, chronological default with a "Past gigs" divider row, sticky headers) but reads its data from a plain `rows` array the caller passes in — the module doesn't fetch, so it works on any page without new endpoints. Optional `artist_name` column links to `/app/artist-profile.html` when `artist_id` is set (city public list). `_no_venue_link` config kills the venue link when the list is scoped to a single venue (nothing new to click). Columns: Date / Venue / City / Start / End. Export CSV includes Artist column when any row has one; PDF is landscape print-to-PDF with the same visual. **Wiring**: **artist public profile** ([artist-profile.html](app/artist-profile.html)) — button in the calendar toolbar, `openArtistPublicGigsList()` shapes `artistGigs` (one row per BOOKED slot the artist holds) + `artistExternalGigs` (external gigs the artist logged themselves). **City public calendar** ([public-gigs.html](app/public-gigs.html)) — button uses `window._pgFilteredGigs()` exposed from public-gigs.js so current filter state (city, radius, artist type) applies to the list; expands multi-slot into one row per slot with artist chip. **Venue public profile** ([venue-profile.html](app/venue-profile.html)) — same shape as city but suppresses venue link (already on the venue's page) and pulls city/state from `venueData`. Cache-busters: `public-gigs-list-modal.js?v=1` (new).

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig flyer upload feature):** Artists can now attach a flyer image to any external gig — completes the "public calendar reflects my real schedule" story. **DB** ([db.py:2287](backend/db.py#L2287)): three new columns on `artist_external_gigs` via `_add_columns` — `flyer_path TEXT`, `flyer_public INTEGER DEFAULT 1`, `flyer_uploaded_at TIMESTAMP`. **Endpoints** ([artist_external_gigs.py](backend/routes/artist_external_gigs.py)): `POST /api/artists/{aid}/external-gigs/{eid}/flyer` (multipart, 3 MB cap, JPG/PNG only — content-type whitelist prevents SVG XSS + oversized memory blowup), `DELETE ...` (unlinks file + clears DB), `GET ...` (auth'd), `GET /api/public/artists/{aid}/external-gigs/{eid}/flyer` (public, gated on `flyer_public=1`). File storage at `/opt/gigsfill/uploads/ext_flyers/{artist_id}/{ext_gig_id}.{jpg|png}`; directory pre-created + `www-data:www-data` owned. `_row_to_dict` normalizes on-disk path → `flyer_url` + `flyer_public_url` so the frontend never sees the file path. Public list endpoint also NULLs out `flyer_path` server-side when `flyer_public=0` for defense-in-depth. **Scheduler cleanup** ([scheduler.py:1670](backend/scheduler.py#L1670)): new `prune_ext_gig_flyers` task in `run_scheduled_emails` unlinks files + clears DB pointers whose gig date is >30 days past. Logged per-run when any pruned. **Frontend create/edit modal** ([artist-book-gigs.html:2593](app/artist-book-gigs.html#L2593)): new Flyer row with drag-drop zone (purple dashed border), file-picker fallback, thumbnail preview + Replace / Remove buttons when a flyer is staged, "Visible to the public" checkbox defaulting ON. Client-side validation: JPG/PNG only, 3 MB max, uses FileReader for pre-upload preview. `_wireFlyerUI()` runs once in `_initChrome`; state (`_flyerPending` / `_flyerRemovePending` / `_flyerCurrentUrl`) resets per modal open. Save flow deferred: main gig POST/PUT commits first, THEN flyer DELETE (if remove was pending), THEN flyer POST (if a new file was staged) — ordered so a replace works. **Frontend view modals**: "🎨 View Event Flyer" button on both the artist book-gigs view modal AND the public artist profile detail modal, wired to a click-to-dismiss dark lightbox overlay. Public button uses `flyer_public_url` (only present when the artist opted in); own-view uses `flyer_url` (auth'd). Cache-busters: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=16`. API + scheduler restarted; verified new columns present + no startup errors.

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig detail modal — full parity with gig-modal.js renderGigModal + public notes exposure):** User provided a screenshot of the Aug 29 real-gig modal on the artist book-gigs calendar and asked "make them all look like this." That modal is `gig-modal.js renderGigModal`, NOT `artist-profile.html showGigDetail` — I'd been matching the wrong reference. **New body renderer** on both surfaces: **rows** — Date / Time / Venue / Location / Artist Type / Lineup / Styles / Notes at font-size 0.95rem with bold labels in `--text-primary`, exactly matching [gig-modal.js:108](app/static/js/gig-modal.js#L108). **Slot card** ([gig-modal.js:1054](app/static/js/gig-modal.js#L1054)): cyan bg `rgba(6,182,212,0.1)` + cyan border `rgba(6,182,212,0.4)` + **purple 3px left stripe** `#a855f7` + "Slot 1" label in purple + time in `#cbd5e1` + "✓ Your Slot" indicator in cyan on the right + inline type-summary as an italic second line ("🎸 Live Band · Full Band · Pop,Rock" in 0.78rem muted italic). No pay pill (user's "minus the pay field"), no Cancel/Countersign actions (external gigs don't have those flows). **Notes placeholder** in the create modal: rewrote to "Enter any pertinent details about this gig for the audience. This information will be visible to the public." — sets the artist's expectation that notes surface publicly. **Backend**: `/api/public/artists/{id}/external-gigs` now returns `notes` + `venue_address` (was redacting both) since the artist explicitly consents by typing into a field labeled "visible to the public". Cache-busters: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=15`. API restarted; verified new fields present in payload.

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig detail modal — byte-for-byte parity with showGigDetail):** User asked "make aug 7 (external gig) look identical to aug 29 minus the pay field." Reverted the inline summary line + reintroduced `Artist Type` and `Band Formats` as labeled `modalRow` entries so the row set exactly matches showGigDetail's multi-slot branch: Date / Venue / Location / Artist Type / Band Formats + red-tinted Slot 1 card. Same for the artist book-gigs view modal (`#extGigViewModal`), with a trailing Notes row kept for the owner-side (notes are personal reference the visitor never sees). No pay field anywhere — the public `/api/gigs/{id}/slots/public` endpoint already redacts financial fields, and external gigs never store pay. Dropped the now-unused `_typeSummary()` helper. Cache-buster: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=14`.

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig detail modal — slot-card font parity + inline type/lineup/style summary):** Two matching-visual asks after user compared the ext modal side-by-side with the regular gig modal. **Slot-card font** on the artist book-gigs view modal (`#extGigViewModal`): the custom `.ext-slot-card` block had `.ext-slot-time { font-size: 0.95rem; }` — visibly larger than the regular `showGigDetail()` slot row's 0.85rem inherited from the row's inline `font-size:0.85rem`. Replaced with the same inline-styled three-column row the regular modal uses ("Slot 1 · 7:00PM – 10:00PM · Fridays Past"), so the font and layout are byte-identical to the reference. **Type / lineup / style rendering** — was a pill-chip row (green rounded chips) below the slot card on the view modal, plus Artist Type / Lineup / Styles as separate label rows on the public profile modal. Now on BOTH surfaces: one inline summary line below the slot card in the "🎸 Live Band · Full Band · Pop,Rock" format the venue slot card + artist book-gigs day modal already use. Muted text, 0.78rem. Redundant standalone rows removed. Cache-buster: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=13`.

- **2026-08-02 (Hover header — slot-preferred artist lookup + external-gig hover artist name):** Two follow-ups after users reported wrong hover text on real booked gigs (Aug 29 "Open" instead of "Booked — Fridays Past") and on external gigs ("Booked" alone instead of "Booked — Fridays Past"). **Root cause on Aug 29** — the gig is flagged `is_multi_slot=1` but only has ONE slot in `gig_slots` (a valid state — single-slot config that's marked as the "new" multi-slot shape). `payloadFromGig()`'s first branch requires `slots.length > 1`, so this gig falls to the SINGLE-slot else branch. That branch reads `g.artist_id` (NULL on multi-slot parents) and `g.artist_name` (NULL when no contract is signed) → `isOpenHere = true` → header renders as "Open" for a booked gig. **Fix** at [gig-hover-card.js:187](app/static/js/gig-hover-card.js#L187): the else branch now prefers `slot0.artist_id / slot0.artist_name / slot0.status` when `g.slots[0]` is present, so a 1-slot multi-slot gig reads the booking off the slot instead of the empty parent. Real single-slot gigs (no slots array) continue to fall through to parent-row logic. **External-gig hover** ([artist-external-gigs.js `_hoverPayload`](app/static/js/artist-external-gigs.js) + [artist-profile.html `_extGigHoverPayload`](app/artist-profile.html)): was `headerPrefix: 'Booked'` + `artistChips: []` — rendered as "Booked" alone. Now sets `headerPrefix: 'Booked — '` and populates artistChips with the current artist's name/id so the hover reads "Booked — Fridays Past" identically to real booked gigs. **External-gig detail modal on public profile** ([artist-profile.html `showExtGigDetail`](app/artist-profile.html)): removed the Time + Artist rows so the row set matches showGigDetail's multi-slot rendering exactly — Date / Venue / Location / Artist Type / Band Formats + slot card (time + artist name are in the slot card, no duplication). Cache-busters: `gig-hover-card.js?v=23` across all 5 pages, `artist-external-gigs.js?v=12`.

- **2026-08-02 (Hover header for booked multi-slot + external-gig details parity):** Two fixes on the details-view surface. **Hover header** ([gig-hover-card.js:143](app/static/js/gig-hover-card.js#L143)): multi-slot booked gigs on the artist public / city public / artist book-gigs calendars were rendering "Booked" alone in the hover header — the venue calendar's per-slot bubbles trimmed slots to length 1 and hit the single-slot path (which already appends the artist name), but the artist/city views pass full multi-slot payloads through payloadFromGig's multi-slot branch which had `artistChips = []` hardcoded with the reasoning "names are in the per-slot breakdown below." Users read the empty header as "Open" for a booked gig. Fixed by populating `artistChips` with the deduped booked/pending artist list per branch (`_uniq(reallyBooked)`, etc.) + suffixing the header with " — " so the render pipeline emits e.g. "Booked — Fridays Past" the way single-slot bookings already do. Dedup by `id|name` composite key so the same artist across multiple slots renders once. Cache-buster: `gig-hover-card.js?v=22` bumped across all 5 pages that load it (venue-profile, public-gigs, venue-create-gigs, artist-profile, artist-book-gigs). **External-gig detail modal on the public artist profile** ([artist-profile.html `showExtGigDetail`](app/artist-profile.html)): rebuilt to match the exact row shape + slot-card visual of the regular `showGigDetail()` — Date / Time / Venue / Location / Artist / Artist Type / Band Formats rows in the same modalRow/modalRowRaw format, followed by a Slot 1 card with the same red-tinted booked-slot treatment (`rgba(239,68,68,0.15)` bg + booked artist name). Dropped the Styles row (regular modal doesn't show it) and the "Lineup" label → "Band Formats" (regular modal convention). Venue rendered as plain text since external venues have no venue_id / no `/app/venue-profile.html` link.

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig — branded delete confirm + venue-modal button conventions):** Polish pass on the buttons/confirms. **Delete confirmation** — was `native confirm('Delete this external gig? This cannot be undone.')`; now `window.showStyledModal` with the same shape as [admin-init.js:680](app/static/js/admin-init.js#L680) (Delete ticket): "Delete this gig?" title, red-highlighted "cannot be undone" body, `[Keep it (ghost), 🗑 Delete (danger)]` buttons, `tone: 'error'`. `_confirmDelete()` helper used by both `deleteExternalGig()` (edit modal) and `deleteExternalGigFromView()` (view modal). **View-modal footer** ([artist-book-gigs.html:#extGigViewModal](app/artist-book-gigs.html)): rename "Delete" → **"Delete Gig"** and drop the `flex: 0 0 auto` on the Delete button so all three (Delete Gig / Edit / Close) stretch equally — matches the venue Create Gig edit-mode footer where Delete Gig / Save Changes / Close all fill `flex: 1`. **Edit-modal footer**: same rename Delete → "Delete Gig", same equal-stretch treatment. **Save-button label** in the edit modal now swaps between "Add Gig" (new gig) and "Save Changes" (editing an existing row) via a new `_setSaveLabel()` helper — matches the venue modal convention at [venue.create-gigs.js:4013](app/static/js/venue.create-gigs.js#L4013). Also fixed the save-button `.finally` block that was hard-coding "Add Gig" on success, so an edit save now restores "Save Changes" correctly. Cache-buster: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=10`.

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig — public profile calendar parity):** Applied the same treatment to the artist's public profile calendar ([artist-profile.html:715](app/artist-profile.html#L715)) as we just gave the artist-book-gigs calendar. Was: muted grey chip with dashed border + "EXT" tag, non-clickable. Now: `.cal-gig.booked` (same styling as any other booked gig on the public profile) rendered as `${icon}${12h time} ${venue}` with the same 6-emoji artist_type icon lookup (🎸/🎧/🎤/🧠/🎙️/🎶). **Hover card** wired via the shared [gig-hover-card.js](app/static/js/gig-hover-card.js) `attachGigHoverCard()` — pass a pre-built `__card:true` payload so the same renderer emits the same visual (headerPrefix "Booked (Non-GigsFill)", date/time block, venue, city/state, lineup + style chips) as booked bubbles. **Click** opens `showExtGigDetail(date, idx)` — a public-safe read-only view reusing the existing `#gigModal` shell with a "ADDED OUTSIDE GIGSFILL" cyan pill at the top + Date / Time / Venue / Location / Artist Type / Lineup / Styles rows. No cache-buster needed (HTML page, not JS include).

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig — click opens a proper details view + click actually fires + MutationObserver loop bug):** Two follow-ups on the previous bubble work. **Click did nothing** — root cause was the MutationObserver watching the calendar with `subtree: true`. Every `container.appendChild(bubble)` inside a `.gigs-container` was a subtree mutation, kicking off inject → mutation → inject at 60fps. The bubble was being destroyed and re-created every frame; the browser's mouse-down/mouse-up landed on different DOM nodes, so click events never registered. Fixed by watching `subtree: false` on the calendar root (artist.book-gigs.js re-renders by appending fresh cells to `#calendar` directly, so we still catch that) plus an `_injecting` reentrancy guard on the callback. **New view modal** ([artist-book-gigs.html:2244 `#extGigViewModal`](app/artist-book-gigs.html#L2244)): click now opens a read-only details view mirroring the visual language of `gig-modal.js renderGigModal` — Date / Time / Venue / Location / Artist Type / Lineup / Styles / Notes rows, plus a purple-tinted Slot 1 card underneath with icon + artist-type + lineup + style chips. "ADDED OUTSIDE GIGSFILL" cyan pill at the top so the artist remembers this row is one they logged themselves. Buttons: Delete (compact left, red-tinted) + Edit (blue primary — opens the existing edit modal) + Close (ghost). `openViewExternalGigModal(g)` and helpers exposed on window; escape / backdrop-click cover both view + edit modals. Cache-buster: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=9`.

- **2026-08-02 (External-gig bubble — visually identical to booked-mine + clickable + state pulldown finally populated):** Two bugs from the previous iteration. **State pulldown was empty** because `us-states.js` declares `US_STATES` with `const` at script top level — that creates a lexical script global, NOT a `window` property. My `_initChrome()` checked `window.US_STATES`, silently missed, and never populated the options. Fixed by referencing `US_STATES` directly via a `typeof` guard, matching the pattern signup-new-init.js already uses. **External-gig calendar bubble** now renders IDENTICALLY to a booked-mine bubble (per user: "Since this is a booked gig they added it, it should be Blue!"). Was a grey dashed-border chip with `19:00 Venue Name` + an "EXT" tag pill; now a `.gig.booked-mine` element (picks up the electric-cyan gradient at [artist-book-gigs.html:656](app/artist-book-gigs.html#L656)) with the same `${icon} ${12h time} · ${venue}` text format as artist.book-gigs.js:1091, same 6-emoji icon lookup keyed on artist_type (🎸/🎧/🎤/🧠/🎙️/🎶). Time formatted 12-hour via a local copy of `formatTime12Hour` (the original is scoped inside the artist.book-gigs.js IIFE, so not reusable). **Click handler** switched from `d.onclick =` bare identifier `openEditExternalGigModal` to `addEventListener('click', ...)` calling `window.openEditExternalGigModal` explicitly — user reported "nothing happens" on click; the bare identifier resolution was unreliable in some browser contexts. `e.stopPropagation()` prevents the parent `cell.onclick` (which opens the "Add" modal on empty-day clicks) from firing on bubble click. Cache-buster: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=7`.

- **2026-08-01 (External-gig modal — state-select chevron fix + venue-style time-flip prompt):** Two follow-ups. **State pulldown white/empty rendering** — turning `appearance: auto` on `<select>` inside the modal was letting the OS chrome bleed through (Chrome on light-theme macOS/Windows renders the native picker as a white button regardless of `background-color`). Fixed by keeping the site's standard `appearance: none` + custom chevron treatment ([gigsfill.css:444](app/static/css/gigsfill.css#L444)) but pinning the `background-color: #151b28 !important` explicitly (avoiding the `background:` shorthand which clobbers the chevron `background-image` layer) and giving the select its own `#333` border + full width + 36px right padding for the chevron. Rules scoped to `#extGigModal select` so they don't fight page selects. **Time-flip prompt** — user asked for the same "did you mean..." UX the venue Create Gig modal has ([venue.create-gigs.js:1279](app/static/js/venue.create-gigs.js#L1279)). Silent auto-flip replaced with `window.showStyledModal` prompt: same title ("🕐 Did you mean..."), same suggestion body ("_9:00 PM to 12:00 AM?_"), same two-button non-dismissible action pair ("No, keep it" / "Yes, fix it"). Two branches match venue's logic: `eh < 12` → PM typo (add 12h), `eh >= 12` → overnight typo (subtract 12h — so 9 PM to 12 PM becomes 9 PM to 12 AM per the user's example). `showStyledModal` provided by `gf-modals.js?v=8` which is already loaded on the artist page. Ported `_fmt12` + `_addHours` helpers directly (kept self-contained rather than reaching into venue.create-gigs.js which isn't loaded here). Cache-buster: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=5`.

- **2026-08-01 (External-gig modal — venue-Create-Gig button chrome + artist-profile pre-fill + state-select fix):** Third follow-up on the external-gig modal. **Button footer** now matches the venue Create Gig modal exactly: `.btn.primary` is solid `#3b82f6` blue, `.btn.ghost` is transparent-with-border, both stretch `flex:1` so they read as full-width paired actions with a divider line above (rules scoped to `#extGigModal .modal-actions` so they don't leak). Delete stays as a compact aside on the left with red-tinted styling. Label swapped from "Cancel" → "Close" to match the venue modal. **State select rendering** — user reported it rendering white/empty. Root cause: the global stylesheet's `select` rule was letting the OS chrome bleed through on the modal. Fixed with the same treatment [signup-new.html](app/signup-new.html) uses on its selects — `#extGigModal select { background: #151b28 !important; border: 1px solid #333 !important; color-scheme: dark; }` plus a matching `option { background:#151b28; color:#fff; }`. Also normalized every text input + textarea inside the modal to the same `#151b28` fill so the whole form reads as one consistent surface. **Modal width** bumped to 720px to match the screenshot proportions the user shared. **Artist Type / Lineup / Styles pre-fill** ([artist-book-gigs-init.js:1082](app/static/js/artist-book-gigs-init.js#L1082)): `initExternalGigs()` now takes a third `defaults` arg wired from the artist's saved profile — `artist_type` scalar, plus `band_formats` + `styles` split from their comma-separated columns on the `artists` table (already returned by the public `GET /api/artists/{id}` endpoint). Applied on ADD only (edit mode uses the row's stored values). Result: a Live Band artist tagged Solo/Duo/Trio/Full Band + Rock never has to re-tag their external gigs — the pills open pre-selected and they can just save. **Tag pill palette** shifted from purple to green when active (`rgba(34,197,94,.20)` bg / `#86efac` text) so it visually reads as the same "confirmed selection" chip the venue's Create Gig Lineup buttons use. Cache-busters: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=4`, `artist-book-gigs-init.js?v=14`.

- **2026-08-01 (External-gig modal — venue-Create-Gig visual match + shared GfDatePicker + tag pills):** Restyle pass so the external-gig create/edit modal reads as part of the same design system as the venue Create Gig modal. **Chrome** ([artist-book-gigs.html:2244](app/artist-book-gigs.html#L2244)): switched from an ad-hoc grid to `.modal.modal-clean` + `.modal-header-row` + `.modal-separator` (purple→cyan gradient bar) + `.modal-section` + `.modal-row` (the same class stack the venue Create Gig modal uses via [modals-legacy.css](app/static/css/modals-legacy.css)). Consequence: label-column, control-column, spacing, typography, and border radii now match without duplicating any CSS. **Dark date picker** ([gf-date-picker.js](app/static/js/gf-date-picker.js), new): extracted `GfDatePicker` (was scoped inside venue.create-gigs.js's IIFE) into a shared module with self-injected CSS. Exposes `window.GfDatePicker`. External-gig modal wires it to the date field so the calendar popup reads dark instead of the browser's native white picker. Also grew `getISO()` / `setISO()` helpers so callers can serialize/populate from yyyy-mm-dd without touching the mm/dd/yyyy display format. **State + city + venue autocomplete** stay from the earlier morning pass; venue-name picks now correctly write into the `<select>` even when the option list hasn't loaded yet (graceful fallback injects the code as an option). **Tag pills** (Artist Type / Lineup / Styles) matching the venue Create Gig filter row's visual: 6-option Artist Type row (Live Band / DJ / Comedian / Trivia Host / Open Mic MC / Karaoke MC) is single-select; when Live Band is active, Lineup (Solo/Duo/Trio/Full Band) + Styles (8 genres from the shared `_VCG_STYLE_OPTIONS` list) reveal. Deactivating Live Band clears those selections so a Solo/DJ payload doesn't silently keep Rock+Jazz+Full-Band tags. **Backend + DB**: three new columns on `artist_external_gigs` — `artist_type TEXT`, `lineup_json TEXT`, `styles_json TEXT` (via `_add_columns` in [db.py:2280](backend/db.py#L2280)). Validation in [artist_external_gigs.py `_validate_payload`](backend/routes/artist_external_gigs.py#L28) whitelists each tag value (an artist can't inject an arbitrary string) and stores lineup/styles as JSON arrays. `_row_to_dict` expands them back to lists on read so the frontend doesn't have to parse. Existing rows read as empty arrays for the tag fields. **Times** default 19:00/22:00 with silent +12h flip on end-blur when end < start. **Small fix from the last iteration**: initial `_add_columns` call used tuple format `(name, type)` which the helper doesn't accept — silently swallowed by its try/except so the columns never landed and the API 500'd on first list. Fixed to the string format the rest of the codebase uses. Cache-busters: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=3`, `gf-date-picker.js?v=1` (new). API restarted; schema migration verified via `PRAGMA table_info`.

- **2026-08-01 (External-gig modal — smart autocomplete + address required + PM time defaults):** Follow-up polish to the artist external-gig feature. **Modal chrome** ([artist-book-gigs.html:2244](app/artist-book-gigs.html#L2244)): header now reads **"ADD GIG NOT BOOKED WITH GIGSFILL:"** and the intro copy sells the "public calendar so your fans know about all of your upcoming gigs" angle; save button label changed from "Save Gig" → "Add Gig". **Field re-order**: Address moved above City (was optional, now **required** — validated both client-side in [artist-external-gigs.js saveExternalGig](app/static/js/artist-external-gigs.js) and server-side in [artist_external_gigs.py _validate_payload](backend/routes/artist_external_gigs.py#L28)). City + State merged onto one row via a flex container. **State** switched from free-text input to a `<select>` populated from `window.US_STATES` on first modal open (`_initChrome`). **City autocomplete**: `initCityAutocomplete({ inputId:'extGigVenueCity', stateId:'extGigVenueState' })` from [city-autocomplete.js](app/static/js/city-autocomplete.js) — same city-with-state-auto-fill behavior artists already know from the demo-request + signup + venue-edit flows. **Venue-name autocomplete**: new `_wireVenueAutocomplete()` fetches `/api/venues/public` on first keystroke (≥2 chars), renders up to 8 matches in a dropdown attached inside the modal (position:absolute, z-index:100), keyboard nav (↑/↓/Enter/Esc), and on pick auto-fills address/city/state from the selected venue. Required backend tweak: `GET /api/venues/public` now returns `address_line_1` alongside city/state ([venues.py:202](backend/routes/venues.py#L202)) — same fields already visible on public venue profiles, so no new PII exposure. **Time defaults**: start=19:00, end=22:00 pre-filled since live-music gigs default to evening. **PM auto-flip**: `_maybeFlipEndToPM()` fires on end-time blur — if `end < start` AND `end < 12`, silently pushes end +12h (so a typed "10:00" after a 19:00 start becomes 22:00 = 10 PM). Kept silent (no modal prompt like the venue create-gig flow) since this modal is lighter and the defaults already point at PM. **Chrome init is one-shot** via `_initedChrome` flag; venue-cache lazy-loaded once. Cache-busters: `artist-external-gigs.js?v=2`, `city-autocomplete.js?v=3` (added to artist-book-gigs.html). API restarted; `/api/venues/public` verified returning the new `address_line_1` key.

- **2026-07-25 (Admin demo requests — past-slot blackout + 6 AM daily digest + multi-invite team members + logo-slot consolidation):** Four bundled follow-ups. **1) Past demo-request time slots blackened on admin panel** ([demo_requests.py:88 new `_slot_is_past`](backend/routes/demo_requests.py#L88), applied both server-side + surfaced client-side): `GET /api/admin/demo-requests` now returns `preferred_slots_past` + `admin_suggested_slots_past` boolean arrays alongside the existing `_human` arrays. `_slotChip` in [admin-demos.js:104](app/static/js/admin-demos.js#L104) renders past slots as a non-interactive black chip with strike-through label + "Past" pill, replacing the purple Accept button, so admin can't accidentally accept a time that's now in the past. Defense-in-depth server guards on BOTH accept paths — the panel accept ([admin_accept_from_panel](backend/routes/demo_requests.py#L1413)) throws 400 and the email-link one-click accept ([accept_via_link](backend/routes/demo_requests.py#L1225)) renders a "⏰ That time has passed" branded page — so a stale tab / stale inbox link can't bypass the UI gate. Pacific-tz comparison since demo slots are all Pacific by design. **2) Daily 6 AM admin reminder for unresponded demo requests** ([send_admin_pending_demo_digest](backend/routes/demo_requests.py#L2858)): new function scans `demo_requests WHERE status='pending'`, renders one branded email listing every pending row with the FULL info block (name/email/phone/entity/location/notes) plus per-slot Accept buttons (same signed HMAC tokens as the original "new demo request" email, so admin can respond straight from the email — no login on mobile). Past slots inside the digest render greyed with a "Past" pill (matches panel behavior); rows where ALL slots are past get a red warning banner urging admin to counter-propose. Scheduler entry in [scheduler.py:1633](backend/scheduler.py#L1633) fires only when local Pacific hour == 6 AND `platform_settings.admin_pending_demo_digest_last_date != today`, so it sends exactly once per day even though the scheduler ticks every hour. Skipped cleanly (with a log line) when there are no pending rows. **3) Team-member invitations rewritten multi-recipient** — old single-email `inviteUserModal` on artist-book-gigs + venue-create-gigs replaced with a gf-modal-style form ([artist-book-gigs.html:2163](app/artist-book-gigs.html#L2163) + [venue-create-gigs.html:3239](app/venue-create-gigs.html#L3239)) that takes a multi-line email textarea (comma/space/newline/semicolon separated) + one shared personal message. New backend endpoints `POST /api/entity-users/{artist|venue}/{id}/invite-multiple` ([entity_users.py](backend/routes/entity_users.py)) share `_invite_multiple_users_to_entity()` which parses + de-dupes emails then loops the existing single-invite path, returning per-status counters (sent / already_member / already_pending / email_failed / invalid / other_errors). `personal_message` threaded through `_invite_user_to_entity` + `_send_invitation_email` and injected into the `entity_invitation` template via a new `{{personal_message_block}}` slot ([email_templates.py:854](backend/email_templates.py#L854)) — renders as a purple-bordered callout, HTML-escaped, newlines → `<br>`, collapses to empty string when message is blank. Frontend `entity-users.js` `openInviteModal` / `sendInvitation` rewritten around new field IDs (`inviteEmails`, `inviteMessage`, `inviteEmailCount`); old first/last/phone + email-lookup code removed. Old single `.../invite` endpoint kept intact — reinvite flows still use it. **4) Logo-hero consolidation on artist + venue public profiles** ([artist-profile.html:361 + 30](app/artist-profile.html#L361) and [venue-profile.html:326 + 30](app/venue-profile.html#L326)): the img + gradient-text name were both rendering below each other — redundant when a logo was uploaded. New `.logo-slot` container wraps them; `.has-logo` class toggled by `loadMedia()` when a `logo`/`profile` media row is found — logo present → show image + hide gradient name; logo missing → hide image + render the gradient name centered INSIDE the same 500×140 frame so the box is never empty. Artist page also lost its "Artist Info" tab entirely — type badge + bio + booking-contact moved into the hero directly under the location line (mirrors venue-profile's "About" pattern), and the `#tab-info` panel + its tab button were deleted. Cache-busters: `artist.edit.js?v=22`, `venue.edit.js?v=11`, `user-availability.js?v=6`, `artist-member-availability.js?v=4`. API + scheduler restarted; new endpoints return 401 unauthenticated as expected; template re-sync ran on startup (55 templates populated).

- **2026-07-22 (Demo email dark-theme rebrand + T-batch tweaks):** Full pass unifying every email in the demo-request thread on the same dark card + purple→cyan gradient header that the admin-side emails already used. Then a batch of 7 UI/UX tweaks. **Demo emails**: all five prospect-facing builders in [demo_requests.py](backend/routes/demo_requests.py) — `_build_prospect_confirmation_html`, `_build_decline_email_html`, `_build_admin_side_cancel_email_html`, `_build_prospect_cancel_ack_html`, `_build_prospect_reminder_html` — rewritten from light `#f8f9fa/#fff` chrome to the dark `#0f1419/#151b28` chrome the admin request/confirmation emails already used. Text `#0f172a` → `#e5e7eb`; sub-panels `#f9fafb` → `#0f172a` with `rgba(139,92,246,0.35)` purple borders; muted text `#6b7280` → `#94a3b8`; prior-slot strike-through swapped to the red-tinted dark card pattern (`rgba(239,68,68,0.08)`); cancel button changed from filled white → transparent red-outlined. Outer body across ALL demo emails (admin + prospect + reschedule alert) unified on `#0f1419` via sed sweep. **T1 venue/artist rename → vanity slug auto-migrates**: new helper [`maybe_update_slug_on_rename`](backend/routes/vanity.py#L125) called from [venues.py update_venue](backend/routes/venues.py#L563) and [artists.py update_artist](backend/routes/artists.py#L362). If the CURRENT stored slug matches `slugify(old_name)` (user never customized), regenerate slug from new name and park the old slug in `vanity_url_redirects` for 90 days so previously-shared links keep working. Frontend hook `window.reloadVanityUrl(type, id)` exposed from [vanity-url-editor.js](app/static/js/vanity-url-editor.js) and called from both [venue.edit.js](app/static/js/venue.edit.js) and [artist.edit.js](app/static/js/artist.edit.js) autosave paths after a name field commits — URL updates in place, no page reload. **T2 Payment Method Remove**: solid red inline styling ([venue-create-gigs.html:2882](app/venue-create-gigs.html#L2882)) — `.btn.danger` was transparent-with-red-border; user wanted a filled red like other Delete buttons. **T3 Live Band default sub-options**: the earlier fix was clobbered by `_vcgAutoPopulateFromArtists` firing INSIDE `applyFilters` and resetting pills to only those found in the narrowed artist list. Moved the "activate all Lineup + Style pills" logic to AFTER `applyFilters()` returns and re-called `applyFilters()` a second time so our defaults win ([venue.create-gigs.js:6829](app/static/js/venue.create-gigs.js#L6829)). **T4 Recent Emails popup**: stronger branded preview chrome around the sandboxed iframe — purple→cyan gradient border, "📧 Email preview" uppercase kicker with gradient underline, "This is how your recipient sees this message:" italic caption, box-shadow for depth ([venue-email-center.js:794-813](app/static/js/venue-email-center.js#L794)). The white iframe now reads as an intentional recipient-view mockup (Gmail-compose style), not an unstyled leak. **T5a Payments Time column**: moved to leftmost slot, Date narrowed to 11% (fits "Dec 24, 2026"), Time widened to 15% for "8:00 PM - 11:00 PM" ranges. Column order: Time · Date · Artist · Gig Fee · Platform Fee · Total Paid · Status. **T5b Admin Users delete**: verified code is present + wired in both Platform Settings Users drill-down AND Directory tab Users sub-tab; user needed hard-refresh to pick up new cache-buster. **T6 My Artists Pay override tooltip**: hover text on both dollar + cent inputs: "$0.00 = no override... the override is a FLOOR, not a fixed rate, so if the gig pays more the artist still gets the higher amount." Matches the earlier Frequency tooltip pattern. **T7 Artist Messages delete + un-hide**: the same trash icon already worked on artist page (shared messages.js). Added an "Un-hide" flow: `GET /api/me/messages?include_hidden=1` bypasses the hide filter and returns `is_hidden: true/false` per row; new `POST /api/me/messages/threads/{gig_id}/{artist_id}/restore` endpoint deletes the hide row. Inbox modal header grew a "Show hidden" checkbox toggle; hidden rows render at 55% opacity with a ↩︎ restore button in place of 🗑. Restore is idempotent + per-user (counterparty never affected). Cache-busters: `messages.js?v=7`, `venue-email-center.js?v=8`, `venue-stripe-payment.js?v=15`, `venue.create-gigs.js?v=241`, `venue.edit.js?v=7`, `artist.edit.js?v=18`, `vanity-url-editor.js?v=4`, `my-artists.js?v=5`. API restarted; new /restore + include_hidden endpoints return 401 unauthenticated as expected.

- **2026-07-21 (Admin Directory tab):** New top-level **Directory** tab in the admin panel (between Platform Settings and Payments) with three sub-tabs — Users / Artists / Venues — each showing every row with rich aggregations that the old Platform Settings drill-down bubbles never surfaced. **Backend** ([admin.py:3336-3475](backend/routes/admin.py#L3336)): three new endpoints `GET /api/admin/directory/{users|artists|venues}` return payloads with correlated-subquery aggregations. Users get `artist_count`, `venue_count`, `gigs_booked_as_artist`, `gigs_posted_as_venue`, `lifetime_venue_spend_cents`, `lifetime_artist_earnings_cents`, plus phone, is_admin, last_login. Artists get band_formats, styles, avg_rating, review_count, gigs_booked, lifetime_payouts_cents, stripe_connect_ok (via `entity_payment_settings.stripe_connect_onboarding_complete`), owner name/email. Venues get venue_size, default_pay, avg_rating, review_count, gigs_posted, gigs_booked, lifetime_spend_cents, payment_method_ok, zero_pay_booking_count, payment_status, owner name/email. Endpoint defensively detects `zero_pay_booking_count` column existence at query time (older dev DBs may not have the migration) and swaps the expression so it never 500s. New endpoints don't touch the existing `/api/admin/{users|artists|venues}` — those keep powering the Platform Settings bubbles unchanged. **Frontend** ([admin-directory.js](app/static/js/admin-directory.js) — new file, 300 lines) implements per-type state (cache, filter, sort, pagination), sub-tab switching scoped to `#directory-tab` (avoids the `.ps-subtab` global-selector bug), search input filters across all keys, click-column-header to sort with visual indicator, row-click opens a detail modal showing every column PLUS extra keys (ID, admin flag, owner ID, payment status). Detail modal includes an "Open Profile ↗" button that deep-links to `/app/artist-profile.html?artist_id=X` or `/app/venue-profile.html?venue_id=X` (public profile pages — always viewable by admin, unlike the edit pages which are owner-only). Users sub-tab keeps the trash icon from the [Jul 21 admin-Users delete work](backend/routes/admin.py#L3336) — clicking fires the same `adminDeleteUser` handler already defined in admin-platform.js. Sub-tab labels show live row counts (e.g. "Users (127)") once the fetch resolves. **HTML** ([admin.html:1414-1466](app/admin.html#L1414)): Directory tab button added to the top nav; tab-content panel with three sub-tab sections. `switchTab('directory')` hook added to the consolidated switchTab override to call `_dirActivate()` on entry. Cache-busters: `admin-directory.js?v=1` (new). API restarted; all three endpoints return 401 unauthenticated. Old Platform Settings bubbles + drill-down remain — Directory is additive, not a replacement.

- **2026-07-21 (Punch-list batch 3 — finish everything):** Three items closed. **#9c 1099-NEC section removed** from Legal/Taxes → Tax Settings sub-tab ([venue-create-gigs.html:3162-3179 deleted](app/venue-create-gigs.html#L3162), [venue-create-gigs-init.js:731-949 replaced with a stub comment](app/static/js/venue-create-gigs-init.js#L731) removing ~220 lines of `generate1099s` / `load1099s` / `view1099` / `send1099` / `sendAll1099s`). Rationale: the Payments-tab banner already tells venues Stripe (TPSO) issues 1099-Ks directly to artists — the 1099-NEC generator contradicted that and created legal/tax confusion. Backend endpoints in [tax.py:402-635](backend/routes/tax.py#L402) (`POST /api/venues/{id}/generate-1099s` etc.) are LEFT REGISTERED (auth-gated, no UI entry) for legacy record recovery if a venue ever needs to pull historical 1099 rows. Affiliate W-9 flow untouched — that's a separate genuine requirement (GigsFill pays affiliates directly and DOES issue 1099-NECs on affiliate earnings ≥ $600). **#4 Venue Messages per-line delete** — hide-for-me (per-user), not hard-delete. New table `gig_message_hides(user_id, gig_id, artist_id, hidden_at)` created inline in [messages.py:606](backend/routes/messages.py#L606) with idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`.
