New endpoint `DELETE /api/me/messages/threads/{gig_id}/{artist_id}` ([messages.py:719](backend/routes/messages.py#L719)) upserts a hide row (DELETE-then-INSERT pattern, portable across SQLite/Postgres). Inbox query ([messages.py:704-711](backend/routes/messages.py#L704)) grew a `NOT EXISTS (... WHERE h.hidden_at >= latest.created_at)` filter — thread stays hidden until a NEW message arrives, then re-surfaces automatically. Frontend ([messages.js:466-543](app/static/js/messages.js#L466)) adds a trailing trash column per row + `_msgInboxHideThread` handler that fires the DELETE, splices the row from the local `msgs` array, and re-renders in place with no reload flicker. Counterparty is unaffected — they still see the thread on their side. **#10 Admin Users delete** — [`DELETE /api/admin/users/{target_user_id}`](backend/routes/admin.py#L3336) mirrors the cascade in [me.py:493 delete_account](backend/routes/me.py#L493) but is admin-only (`check_admin`) and takes any target user id. Uses the same `services.entity_delete` helpers (`_assert_no_charged`, `_delete_artist`, `_delete_venue`) so behavior stays identical to self-delete: notify booked-gig counterparties, mark in-flight transactions as `account_deleted`, wipe every child table (email_preferences → support_tickets → notifications → entity_users → payment_methods → sms_preferences → affiliate_* → entity_invitations → w9_forms → gig_messages → gig_message_hides → user_settings → user_availability → artist_email_digest_queue), NULL-out reviews' `reviewer_user_id` to preserve aggregated ratings, DELETE the user row, then rmtree filesystem media AFTER commit. Refuses if any charged/pending transaction exists on any owned entity (403 → force refund flow first). Admin cannot delete themselves via this endpoint (400 self-check — use `/api/me/delete` instead). Every delete logs `delete_user` in `admin_audit_log` with the target user snapshot + counts. Frontend ([admin-platform.js:283-380](app/static/js/admin-platform.js#L283)) adds trash column to the Users drill-down table (scoped to `_dsType === 'users'`; artists/venues/gigs unchanged); confirm modal spells out the cascade + refund-blocker before firing. Cache-busters: `messages.js?v=6`, `admin-platform.js?v=11`, `venue-create-gigs-init.js?v=11`. API restarted; both new DELETE endpoints return 401 unauthenticated. Punch-list complete: 15 of 16 items shipped, the 16th (#9c) is a deliberate removal per user decision.

- **2026-07-21 (Punch-list batch 2 — investigation-heavy fixes):** Six more items from the same punch list, several requiring codebase audit before code touched. **#2a hide My Availability tab** for artist-less users ([user-profile.js:389](app/static/js/user-profile.js#L389)): `loadArtistsCount` now toggles the tab button + panel to `display:none` when `artists.length === 0`. Deep-linked `#availability` still resolves to a hidden panel — acceptable since only artist-affiliated users would deep-link there. **#2b Same-Day Booking gatekeeper — REAL FIX** ([gigs.py:945-985 + 2652 + 5218](backend/routes/gigs.py#L945)): the `venue_booking_approval_request` preference was previously ONLY an email mute — the pending_venue_approval hold ALWAYS fired, so venue users who turned it off saw same-day bookings sit forever without notification. New helper `_venue_requires_same_day_approval(db, venue_id)` checks the VENUE OWNER's `email_preferences.enabled` row (0 → auto-confirm, NULL/1 → keep the hold). Wired into both the whole-gig `book` path AND the slot `book_slot` path. UI copy in [user-profile.js:869](app/static/js/user-profile.js#L869) rewritten so the toggle's real effect is stated on the tin. Preferred artists still bypass entirely (they always have). **#2c Preferred approved/denied/revoked scoping** — verified via source audit ([preferred_artists.py:1039/1054/1190/1205/1330/1345](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py#L1039)): six distinct notification_types (`artist_preferred_*` vs `venue_preferred_*`), each dispatched separately per user, gated on that user's `email_preferences` row. Toggling the venue's off has ZERO effect on the artist's email. No bug, no change. **#5 lightbox close** — venue-profile lightbox had NO close mechanism at all (no ✕ button, no backdrop click, no Escape) because the inline `openModal` was the only one running — the "handled by venue-profile-init.js" comment referenced a script that never loads. Fixed [venue-profile.html:401 + 1221-1252](app/venue-profile.html#L401): added ✕ button in `#mediaModal`, backdrop-click closes on `e.target === modal`, global Escape listener, `closeMediaModal()` wipes the iframe/video so YouTube stops playing. [artist-profile.html:1176](app/artist-profile.html#L1176) already had ✕ + backdrop-click; added Escape for parity. **#6 Live Band default sub-options** ([venue.create-gigs.js:6813](app/static/js/venue.create-gigs.js#L6813)): when the Live Band artist-type pill goes from inactive→active, every `.band-format-toggle` + `.style-toggle` pill now auto-activates via `_vcgSetPillActive(b, true)`; when Live Band goes active→inactive, they auto-deactivate (so hidden sub-filters don't lurk affecting a subsequent DJ/Comedian search). Clear Filters + click Live Band again produces the same default-all behavior. **#7 Frequency override UI bug** ([my-artists.js:531 + 569](app/static/js/my-artists.js#L531)): pay override defaults to 0/00 (matches user intent — backend treats pay 0 ≡ NULL), but frequency override was ALSO defaulting to 0 on render — and backend semantics differ for freq (0 = "no gap required", NULL = "inherit venue default"). A venue tabbing through the freq input silently converted "use venue default" into "artist can book anytime". Fixed: NULL freq_override → blank input value; added `placeholder="—"` and a `title=` explaining the three states. `saveOverride` already converts `'' → null`, so no server change needed. **#8 Email Center Recent Emails popup** ([venue-email-center.js:793-826](app/static/js/venue-email-center.js#L793)): sandboxed email-body iframe was a stark white rectangle floating in the dark modal. Wrapped in a branded dark panel with a "📧 Email preview" uppercase label + subtle border + rounded corners. The iframe's white background is now legible as an intentional "recipient sees this" preview surface. Cache-busters: `user-profile.js?v=16`, `my-artists.js?v=4`, `venue.create-gigs.js?v=240`, `venue-email-center.js?v=7`. API restarted; no errors. **Deferred still**: #4 (venue Messages per-line delete), #10 (admin Users delete) — both need design decisions. **Awaiting user decision**: #9c 1099-NEC section fate (my recommendation follows in the response).

- **2026-07-21 (Punch-list batch 1: venue-rename 500 hotfix + sender switch + notifications restyle + Payment Method polish):** Six items shipped from a large user punch-list; the remaining ~7 investigation items are still pending. **#3 CRITICAL** — venue-edit page silently 500ing on every save since yesterday's rename-propagation change ([venues.py:396-411](backend/routes/venues.py#L396)). `SELECT v.stripe_customer_id` referenced a column that doesn't exist on `venues`; it lives on `entity_payment_settings` keyed by `(entity_type='venue', entity_id)`. Fixed with a `LEFT JOIN entity_payment_settings eps` and pull `eps.stripe_customer_id` instead. Nobody could rename a venue for ~14 hours. Once fixed, the propagation to public page / browser tab / My Venues (yesterday's Jul 20 work) works as designed. **#11 sender switch** — `platform_settings` UPDATEd: `platform_email = 'support@gigsfill.com'` (was `booked@gigsfill.com`), `platform_email_from_name = 'GigsFill Support'` (was `GigsFill Booking`). User confirmed mailbox + password work on `mail.gigsfill.com:26`. Takes effect immediately since `EmailService` is instantiated per-request. Every outbound email now reads "GigsFill Support <support@gigsfill.com>". Demo-request admin RECIPIENT still `jcarta@gigsfill.com` (that's a separate `demo_request_admin_email` setting the user didn't ask to change). **#2d Venue Blast Emails heading** ([user-profile.js:911-921](app/static/js/user-profile.js#L911)) promoted to match the page's top "Notification Preferences" style (1rem, `.gradient-title` class, uppercase, 0.08em letter-spacing) with `margin: 32px 0 8px 0` for breathing room. **#2e Artist Notifications heading** added at the top of the artist section, mirroring the existing "Venue Notifications" heading (cyan 0.9rem uppercase) so both sub-sections read as parallel. **#9a Payment Method Update button** ([venue-create-gigs.html:2881](app/venue-create-gigs.html#L2881)) `.btn.ghost` → `.btn.primary` (brand purple). Remove stayed `.btn.danger` (already red-branded). **#9b Payments tab Time column** now shows the full gig start-end range ("8:00 PM - 11:00 PM") instead of just start. Endpoint ([stripe_connect.py:1967](backend/routes/stripe_connect.py#L1967)) now returns `g.end_time as gig_end_time`; renderer ([venue-stripe-payment.js:226-244](app/static/js/venue-stripe-payment.js#L226)) formats both times through a shared `_fmt12` helper and concatenates with ` - ` when end is present. **Deferred**: #4 (venue Messages inbox per-line delete) + #10 (admin Users tab delete) both need real design decisions before implementation — per-user hide vs hard-delete for #4, full cascade design for #10 (mirrors the ~200-line self-delete flow in `me.py:493-720`). Cache-busters: `user-profile.js?v=15`, `venue-stripe-payment.js?v=14`. API restarted; no errors in log.

- **2026-07-20 (Admin Payments polish + Help/Feedback auto-close + support-reply signature drop):** Four small follow-ups. **1) Payments tab hero snapshot** ([admin-payments.js:180-205](app/static/js/admin-payments.js#L180)): after yesterday's merge collapsed the accounting bubbles into the tight ops KPI strip, the "at a glance" feel was lost. Added a `renderHeroStats` function that renders **5 large sub-labelled bubbles** — Completed Txns (with "N successful" subline) / Total Gig Value (with "avg $X" subline) / Total Fees ("platform revenue") / Stripe Costs ("processing") / Net Profit ("after Stripe") — into a new `#apHeroStats` container above the ops strip. Backend now also returns `gig_value_count` from `/api/admin/payments/stats` ([admin_payments.py:472-476](backend/routes/admin_payments.py#L472)) so avg per gig has an exact denominator (paid+charged+pending_transfer+transfer_failed rows). Reverted the 3 tighter bubbles (Gig Value / Stripe Costs / Net Profit) I'd added to the ops KPI strip yesterday since they'd now duplicate the hero row — KPI strip is back to its original 8 counters. **2) Payments table rework** ([admin-payments.js:325-390](app/static/js/admin-payments.js#L325)): Stripe column removed (the pi_/tr_ ID + Dashboard link still live in the row-detail modal on click); Gig Date moved to the far-left data slot (first column after the checkbox) to match the new default sort. Backend `/api/admin/payments/search` `ORDER BY` swapped to `g.date DESC, g.start_time DESC, t.id DESC` — most recent gigs at the top — matching the user's mental model when they open the page. **3) Help + Feedback modals auto-close after success** ([user-dropdown.js:558-568, 632-641](app/static/js/user-dropdown.js#L558)): both modals hit an "OK to dismiss" wall after "✓ Ticket submitted!" / "✓ Thanks!" success — the user had to click OK even though there was nothing left to do. Added a 3-second `setTimeout` that calls the respective close function, guarded by an `.open` class check so it's a no-op if the user already closed manually. Success message stays visible for the full 3s. **4) Support reply email drops "- {{admin_name}}" signature line** ([email_templates.py:2586-2589](backend/email_templates.py#L2586)): the trailing `- John Carta` line under the response body read as odd/redundant (admin's name is already in the header + email metadata). Line removed from `support_ticket_reply`. `admin_name` variable still passed in template vars + still stored on the reply row (used by the admin-side threaded reply list), just not rendered in the outbound email. Startup `_populate_email_templates()` re-synced the DB copy on restart (verified via `instr(body, 'admin_name') = 0`). Cache-busters: `admin-payments.js?v=28`, `user-dropdown.js?v=10` (bumped across all 10 pages that load it). API restarted; `/stats` + `/search` both still `401` unauthenticated.

- **2026-07-20 (Admin panel — merged Payment Accounting into the Payments tab):** The **Payment Accounting** sub-tab under Platform Settings is retired; its summary bubbles + Export button now live on the top-level **Payments** tab, so all payment-side surfaces are in one place. **Backend** ([admin_payments.py:459-539](backend/routes/admin_payments.py#L459)): extended `GET /api/admin/payments/stats` to also return `gig_value_cents` (sum of `gig_fee_cents` on completed non-cancelled rows), `stripe_fees_cents`, and `net_profit_cents` (`gigsfill_profit_cents`), each honoring the existing `from_date` / `to_date` window params. Cancelled rows contribute their `platform_fee_on_cancel_cents` to commission (mirroring the retired accounting page's math) but nothing to gig value. **Frontend Payments KPI strip** ([admin-payments.js:180-206](app/static/js/admin-payments.js#L180)): three new bubbles (Gig Value / Stripe Costs / Net Profit) sit between the Payouts and status counters. **Header**: 📥 Export button added left of Reconcile / Refresh — opens the same `#accountingExportModal` (CSV + printable HTML with per-row Venue Fee / Artist Fee / Venue Charged / Stripe Fee / GF Profit columns + totals row) that the retired sub-tab used. **Retired code** ([admin-init.js:704-725](app/static/js/admin-init.js#L704)): deleted `loadAccounting`, `renderAccountingSummary`, `renderAccountingTable`, `acctSortBy`, `acctGoPage` and their pagination/sort state (`_acctSort`, `_acctPage`, `ACCT_PER_PAGE`) — ~175 lines. Preserved `_acctData` (now lazy-loaded on export-modal open via `showAccountingExportModal` since nothing pre-populates it) plus `_fmt12` / `_cents` helpers that the CSV/print emitter still uses. `switchPsTab('accounting')` hook removed. **Rationale**: same data source, but the Payments table already renders all row-level detail with better filters, drill-down, refund, reconcile, bulk actions; the only unique value the old page added was the aggregate math + export, both of which are now in Payments. Bonus: the merged KPIs honor the Payments date filter, so admin can slice financials by window instead of the old all-time-only view. Cache-busters: `admin-init.js?v=6`, `admin-payments.js?v=27`. API restarted; smoke-tested `/api/admin/payments/stats` + `/api/admin/accounting` still return 401 unauthenticated (both remain `check_admin`-gated).

- **2026-07-20 (Admin panel — support-ticket trash icon + Affiliates sub-tab highlight fix):** Two small admin-side follow-ups. **1) Help & Support Tickets now has a trash column** at the far right, mirroring the Contact Messages row layout — subtle grey 🗑 that turns red on hover, `event.stopPropagation()` so it doesn't open the ticket modal. Click fires a branded `showStyledModal` confirm ("Permanently delete <subject> from <user> and all its replies? This cannot be undone. To keep the audit trail, mark it closed instead.") → `DELETE /api/admin/support-tickets/{id}`. New endpoint in [admin.py:1691-1717](backend/routes/admin.py#L1691): cascades to `support_ticket_replies` first (no FK trigger on SQLite), then deletes the ticket, then logs `delete_support_ticket` via `log_admin_action` with the pre-delete row snapshot (id / subject / user_email / status) for the audit trail. Local `allSupportTickets` / `filteredSupportTickets` arrays are pruned client-side for instant feedback, then `loadSupportTickets()` re-runs so the `supportTicketsBadge` and top-level Messages badge re-sum. **2) Affiliate Accounting sub-tab wasn't highlighting on click** — root cause in [admin-affiliate.js](app/static/js/admin-affiliate.js) `switchAffTab`: the un-scoped selector `document.querySelector('.ps-subtab[onclick*="accounting"]')` returned *Payment Accounting* (line 935 in admin.html — earlier in DOM, on the Payments tab) instead of *Affiliate Accounting* (line 1708), so the `.active` class never landed on the right button. Fix scopes both the querySelectorAll (removes) and the querySelector (add) to `#affiliates-tab`, matching the pattern used in `switchMessagesSubTab`. *Affiliate Settings* worked before the fix only because no other sub-tab on the page had `settings` in its onclick — a lucky no-collision, not a working design. Cache-busters bumped: `admin-init.js?v=4`, `admin-affiliate.js?v=3`. Server restart required for the DELETE endpoint to register (`sudo systemctl restart gigsfill`); JS + HTML changes are live on refresh. Smoke-tested unauthenticated DELETE returns 405 pre-restart (URL matches, method unknown to the current process) → will be 401 after restart per the `Depends(check_admin)` gate.

- **2026-07-20 (Admin panel — Messages tab consolidation + DB/Logs default):** Top-level `Demo Requests` tab is gone; `Support` tab renamed to **Messages** and grew three sub-tabs: **Demo Requests** / **Help & Support Tickets** / **Contact Messages** (in that visual left→right order). Sub-tab switcher is a new `switchMessagesSubTab(name)` scoped to `#messages-tab` (uses the existing `.ps-subtab` / `.ps-subtab-content` classes). On tab entry, `_messagesActivate()` fires all three loaders in parallel (`loadDemoRequests`, `loadSupportTickets`, `loadContactMessages`), then picks the leftmost sub-tab whose per-tab badge shows a non-zero count as the default view; falls back to Demo Requests when everything is clean. **Per-sub-tab badges**: `demosPendingBadge` (unchanged id, still counts `status='pending'`), new `supportTicketsBadge` (open-ticket count, computed in `admin-init.js loadSupportTickets`), new `contactMessagesBadge` (contact rows with `status='new'`, computed in `admin-contact.js loadContactMessages`). Every loader that touches a badge now also calls `window._updateMessagesTotalBadge()`, which sums the three and writes to a **new top-level `messagesTabBadge`** — the number next to "Messages" in the tab bar reflects the grand total of unread action items. Analytics stat card "Open Tickets" now navigates to `Messages → Help & Support Tickets` instead of the retired Support tab. **DB/Logs tab default fix**: `switchPsTab` uses a global `.ps-subtab-content` selector that clobbers the DB/Logs sub-tab state — added an explicit `switchDbLogsTab('database')` call inside the main `switchTab` handler for `tab === 'dblogs'` so Database is always the landing sub-tab (previously nothing defaulted; user had to click). **Deep-link handlers updated**: `?tab=demos` and `?tab=messages` both route to `switchTab('messages') + switchMessagesSubTab('demos')`; `?reply=<id>` on contact-messages URL opens `Messages → Contact Messages`. Cache-busters bumped: `admin-demos.js?v=19`, `admin-contact.js?v=11`, `admin-init.js?v=3`. Backend unchanged — pure frontend restructure; smoke-tested `/api/admin/demo-requests`, `/api/admin/support-tickets`, `/api/admin/contact-messages` all still `401` unauthenticated. See [admin.html](app/admin.html) for the tab HTML + inline switcher/aggregator functions.

- **2026-07-20 (Venue / artist rename now propagates everywhere — 5 stale-name fixes):** Audit-driven cleanup so a name change in profile settings flows to every user-visible surface. **#1 Stripe Customer.name** ([venues.py:405-411, 537-556](backend/routes/venues.py#L405)): venue rename now calls `stripe.Customer.modify(cid, name=new_name)` after commit, best-effort with warning-log on failure. Previously the Stripe dashboard + hosted invoices kept the old name indefinitely. **#2 Pending gig-contract body** ([contracts.py:508-604](backend/routes/contracts.py#L508)): new `_re_render_body_if_pending(db, contract_row)` helper re-runs the substitution pipeline (auto_generated → `generate_auto_contract`, custom_builder → the replacements-dict loop) against live venue/artist data on GET, and at sign time the fresh body is PERSISTED so signed PDFs capture whatever the names were at signing (not at creation). Signed contracts remain frozen — correct legal behavior. Wired into `get_gig_contract` + `get_gig_contract_by_gig` + the `sign_contract` UPDATE. **#3 Artist invitations** ([me.py:1062-1076, 1161-1175, 1409-1423, 1433-1446](backend/routes/me.py#L1062)): 4 SELECTs on `artist_invitations` now `LEFT JOIN venues v ON v.id = ai.venue_id` and use `COALESCE(v.venue_name, ai.venue_name)` so pending-invite lists show the current venue name. **#4 Flyer thumbnails** ([venues.py:558-573, artists.py:288-296, 356-378](backend/routes/venues.py#L558)): on rename, `UPDATE flyers SET thumbnail_data = NULL WHERE (venue_id | artist_id) = :id AND is_template = 0` so the frontend regenerates the PNG from live canvas data on next open. Filename left alone (self-heals on next re-save; only visible on download). **#5 Message thread sender_name** ([messages.py:259-284, 621-635](backend/routes/messages.py#L259)): both the thread-view SELECT and the inbox-listing SELECT now resolve sender name via LEFT JOIN venues/artists keyed on `sender_type + sender_entity_id`, using `COALESCE(v.venue_name, a.name, m.sender_name)` in the row view and a `CASE WHEN sender_type = 'venue' THEN v.venue_name WHEN 'artist' THEN a.name` in the inbox — stored snapshot only used as defensive fallback when the entity is missing. Historical intentionally-frozen fields NOT touched: `tax_1099s` (IRS snapshot), `venue_email_history` (audit log), signed contract PDFs on disk, already-sent email content, `notifications.message`. All endpoints re-verified with unauth smoke tests → correct 401/403 gating. No schema changes required; all fixes are read-time JOIN swaps + one rename-time propagation step.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — pre-launch polish: sort, when-hint, meeting URL warning):** Three small quality-of-life tweaks before real prospects hit the flow. **1) Sort order** in `GET /api/admin/demo-requests`: pending first (needs action), then scheduled with **soonest `scheduled_at` first** (upcoming demos at the top so admin can scan the queue by "what's next"), then everything else by `created_at DESC`. Verified with a 4-row synthetic dataset. **2) When-hint** under the status pill: replaced the always-"requested X ago" corner text with a status-aware `_relScheduled(scheduled_at)` helper that reads "in 2 days" / "tomorrow" / "in 3 hr" for scheduled rows and "yesterday" / "2 wks ago" for completed / no_show; pending / declined / cancelled keep the existing `_relDate(created_at)` reading. Naive-Pacific interpretation is close enough for the admin who's Pacific-based, and the phrasing survives a few-hour drift for out-of-TZ admins. **3) Amber warning banner** in Settings → Demo Pipeline: visible only when `demo_meeting_url` is empty, reading "⚠️ No default meeting URL set — scheduled-demo confirmation + reminder emails will read 'we'll follow up with the video call link before the demo' until you paste a link below". Hides the instant admin types a URL (input-event listener), reappears if cleared. `admin-demos.js?v=15`, `admin-platform.js?v=10`. All three are pure UX — no schema changes, no email changes, no breaking behavior.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — internal notes on the outcome modal):** New `demo_requests.outcome_notes TEXT` column. Outcome modal grows a textarea between the "Was scheduled for" panel and the Completed / No Show chips ("How the demo went, follow-up needed, next steps, etc."), autofocused on open so admin can start typing immediately. Value is grabbed when either chip is clicked and sent alongside the outcome as `PUT /api/admin/demo-requests/{id}/outcome {outcome, outcome_notes}`. Endpoint distinguishes omitted vs empty-string — omitting leaves prior notes untouched, empty string explicitly clears. Notes render on the row card whenever the row is in `completed` or `no_show` status as a cyan-accented block (📝 Notes: …) — visually distinct from the greyscale decline note above (which reflects a message sent to the prospect). Truncated inline with the full text on hover via `title=`. History logs `notes_updated: bool`. Internal only — no email is sent on any outcome or notes change. `admin-demos.js?v=14`. End-to-end verified: stored a note ("Great demo! They booked a follow-up next week.") → switched status completed → no_show while updating notes → notes cleared with NULL — all worked correctly with schema in place.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — auto-complete after the demo passes + click-to-change outcome):** After a scheduled demo's time + 60 min elapses, `mark_completed_demos(cursor)` fires on every scheduler tick (wired into `run_scheduled_emails` in [scheduler.py](backend/scheduler.py) alongside `send_pending_demo_reminders`) and transitions `scheduled → completed`. 60 min = the 45 min demo block + 15 min cushion so we never flip status mid-demo. `history_json` gets an `auto_completed actor=scheduler` entry so the paper trail is clean. Admin panel status pill for `completed` (cyan) and `no_show` (orange) is **clickable** — opens a branded outcome modal with the scheduled time preview + two big option chips (**✓ Completed / Demo happened** vs **✗ No Show / Prospect didn't join**), highlighting whichever is current so admin sees at-a-glance what they'd be changing from. New endpoint `PUT /api/admin/demo-requests/{id}/outcome` accepts `{outcome: 'completed'|'no_show'}`; allows all transitions between `scheduled`, `completed`, `no_show` (so admin can also mark before the auto-completer runs), returns 409 for any other prior status. **Internal use only — no email sent to prospect.** History logs `outcome_set prior_status=... new_status=...`. Filter dropdown extended: All / Pending only / Scheduled / **Completed** / **No Show** / Declined / Cancelled. `admin-demos.js?v=12`. End-to-end verified: inserted past-scheduled row → `mark_completed_demos` returned 1 auto-mark → row.status='completed' + history entry logged → second run returned 0 (idempotent) → scheduler live-picked up the new job on next tick.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — admin can counter-propose 1-3 slots when declining):** Decline modal upgraded from an inline red panel to a branded `showStyledModal` with the prospect's declined times shown struck through at the top + a message textarea + **3 optional slot pickers** (native `<input type="date">` with `color-scheme: dark` + hour/minute selects, same 9 AM–5 PM PT / 15-min-increment constraints as the request submit). Filling any subset of 1-3 rows switches the prospect's decline email from the "Pick new times" fallback path to a **one-click accept** flow: each filled slot renders as a green "✓ Book this time" button linked to `GET /demo/prospect-accept/{token}` — new `URLSafeTimedSerializer(SECRET_KEY, salt="demo-prospect-accept")` 60-day tokens bound to (req_id, slot_index_into_admin_suggested_slots). When prospect clicks, the row moves `declined → scheduled` with the picked slot swapped into `preferred_slots_json[0]` + `slots_version` bumped (stale admin accept-links invalidated) + `scheduled_slot_index=0` — this keeps every downstream reader (reminder scanner, ICS, calendar buttons, confirmation-mode detector) working without per-callsite branches. Full confirmation-to-both-sides flow fires (branded email + 3 calendar buttons + ICS attachment with meeting URL if set); history entry logged as `scheduled actor=prospect_from_decline_email confirm_mode=prospect_accepted_admin_suggestion`. Idempotent — a second click on the same or another suggested slot shows the branded "Already scheduled" page. **Fallback still there**: the "📅 Pick new times" reschedule button renders under the accept cards so the prospect can still counter-counter-propose if none of admin's suggestions fit. New DB column `demo_requests.admin_suggested_slots_json`; admin list endpoint exposes `admin_suggested_slots` + `admin_suggested_slots_human` alongside preferred slots. History entry on the decline itself now captures `suggested_slots` for the audit trail. Verified end-to-end with a synthetic row: decline stored 2 counter-slots → prospect-accept click on slot #1 (Aug 2 14:30 PT) → row scheduled with correct `scheduled_at`, `slots_version=2`, history logged, idempotent second click renders "Already scheduled". `admin-demos.js?v=11`.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — decline email now loops into the picker too):** Decline email drops the "reply to this email with 2-3 additional times" copy and mirrors the cancel path with a branded **📅 Pick new times** button. Reschedule endpoints (`GET/POST /api/demo-request/reschedule/{token}`) relaxed a second time to also accept `declined` rows — same revival semantics as cancelled (row stays put, slots_version bumps, status → pending, `revived_by_prospect prior_status=declined` history entry, `[Demo Revived]` admin subject). Now the ONLY terminal state that closes the thread is a delete. Verified end-to-end with a synthetic declined row: GET returns prefill JSON (was 409), POST swaps slots + revives, history correctly logs the revival with prior_status=declined.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — Teams / Zoom / Meet meeting URL integration):** Prospects can now join demos in-browser with **zero account required** — admin pastes a Teams / Zoom / Meet URL and every confirmation/reminder/calendar-invite gets the link auto-embedded. **Two-tier config**: platform-wide default via Settings → Demo Pipeline → *Default Demo Meeting URL* (`platform_settings.demo_meeting_url`) covers 95% of demos with one URL; per-row override via a **🎥 URL** button on any scheduled row (new `demo_requests.meeting_url` TEXT column + `PUT /api/admin/demo-requests/{id}/meeting-url`) for the one-off cases. Resolution helper `_get_meeting_url(row, db)` returns row override → platform default → empty. When empty, emails render "we'll follow up with the video call link before the demo" and the meeting button on scheduled rows shows amber `🎥 Set URL` as a soft nudge. **Fully wired through**: prospect + admin confirmation emails, prospect + admin morning-of reminder emails, and the ICS attachment (as `LOCATION:` + `URL:` fields — most calendar apps linkify LOCATION so the URL is one click away from the event popover); calendar-link helper embeds the URL into Google Calendar and Outlook add-event deep-links as their location parameter. Admin settings tab picks up two new fields side-by-side: *Demo Request Notify Email* + *Default Demo Meeting URL*; admin-demos list endpoint returns `platform_meeting_url` alongside `requests` so the row buttons can distinguish `URL (custom)` from `URL (default)`. Per-row modal validates `http[s]://` prefix and offers a "Clear override" button when the row has one; empty submit means "use platform default". `history_json` gets a `meeting_url_updated` entry with the prior + new URLs. `admin-demos.js?v=10`, `admin-platform.js?v=9`. Prospect UX: click Google/Outlook button in confirmation email → event lands on calendar with join URL as the location, no extra copy/paste.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — cancel emails now loop back into the picker, no more 'reply with times'):** Cancel emails (both `_build_admin_side_cancel_email_html` when admin cancels, and `_build_prospect_cancel_ack_html` when prospect cancels themselves) drop the "reply to this email with new times" copy and instead surface a branded **📅 Pick new times** gradient button. The button reuses the same `?reschedule=<token>` URL that the confirmation email's Change-time button uses — the reschedule endpoints (`GET/POST /api/demo-request/reschedule/{token}`) are relaxed to also accept `cancelled` rows, treating a click on the button as a revival of the existing thread instead of forcing a fresh homepage request. `_declined` rows stay closed (admin actively said no). Revival is distinguished from reschedule in the history: `history_json` gets a `revived_by_prospect` entry (not `rescheduled_by_prospect`) when `prior_status == 'cancelled'`, and the admin subject swaps `[Demo Rescheduled]` for `[Demo Revived]`. Prospect success message uses "Thanks for coming back" copy for revivals. **Everything stays inside the same row / same paper trail** — prospect's contact + entity_type carry over, admin sees the whole flow in one queue. End-to-end verified: cancelled row → GET reschedule returns prefill JSON (was 409) → POST swaps slots + revives → row=pending / v+1 / history correctly logs `revived_by_prospect prior_status=cancelled`.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — confirmation emails now know if a time was changed):** New helper `_detect_confirm_mode(row)` scans `history_json` and returns one of three modes for the next confirmation email: `'new'` (first booking, no prior scheduled entry), `'admin_changed'` (last transition was `reverted_to_pending`, i.e. admin picked a new slot after picking one they didn't want), or `'prospect_changed'` (last transition was `rescheduled_by_prospect`, i.e. prospect used the Change-time link and admin re-accepted). `_build_prospect_confirmation_html` and `_build_admin_confirmation_html` both take `mode` + `prior_slot` and vary header text, intro copy, and time labels accordingly. Admin-changed prospect email opens with "Sorry for the shuffle — something came up on our end" and shows the old time struck through in a red panel above the new time; prospect-changed email opens with "Thanks for the update — we've confirmed one of your new preferred times". Admin sees a parallel red strike-through banner reading "Remove this from your calendar and add the new one below" so their calendar doesn't end up with duplicate demo entries. Subject-line prefixes also switch: `[Demo Time Changed]` vs `[Demo Rebooked]` vs the original `[Demo Confirmed]`. Detector correctly handles multi-cycle rows (schedule → revert → schedule → prospect-reschedule → schedule) — verified with a 4-entry synthetic history that returns `prospect_changed` + the most recent prior slot. History entries appended at accept time carry the resolved `confirm_mode` and `prior_slot` too for future observability.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — admin-side change/cancel for scheduled rows):** Admin panel now shows two extra buttons on scheduled rows (mirror to the prospect's Change/Cancel in the confirmation email): **↻ Change time** and **✗ Cancel demo**. Two new backend endpoints power them. `POST /api/admin/demo-requests/{id}/revert-to-pending` — for the "I picked the wrong slot" case: clears `scheduled_slot_index`/`scheduled_at`/`reminder_sent_at`, leaves `preferred_slots_json` + `slots_version` alone (stale accept-links from the admin email stay valid), moves status back to `pending` so the 3 accept-slot buttons render again. **Does not email the prospect** — a "your time changed to X" confirmation only fires when admin re-accepts. `POST /api/admin/demo-requests/{id}/cancel` — for the "we actually can't do this demo" case: takes an optional `message` textarea, moves status → `cancelled`, sends the prospect a branded cancel email with the message inline ("Something came up on our end — reply with new times…"), plus history_json entry `cancelled_by_admin`. Distinct from Delete (hard-remove, no email) and Decline (only works on pending rows, different email tone). Both actions use branded `showStyledModal`s with strike-through visual on the released time, purple gradient for Change, red border for Cancel. `admin-demos.js?v=9`. End-to-end verified: revert endpoint responds `{ok:true}` and correctly rejects with 409 if row isn't scheduled; cancel endpoint enforces `pending|scheduled` only and returns 409 otherwise; both properly gated on `check_admin`.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — 1-click Google / Outlook / Apple add-to-calendar + admin post-accept email):** Confirmation emails on both sides now embed a 3-button "Add to your calendar" strip: **Google** (`calendar.google.com/render?action=TEMPLATE` with naive-Pacific dates + `ctz=America/Los_Angeles`), **Outlook** (`outlook.office.com/calendar/deeplink/compose?rru=addevent` with UTC ISO — one host covers personal outlook.com AND corporate O365), **Apple / Other** (signed link to `GET /demo/ics/{token}` that returns the same VCALENDAR with `Content-Disposition: attachment`). Buttons are colored to match brand: Google red, Outlook blue, Apple neutral gray. Third URLSafeTimedSerializer salt `demo-ics` (60-day TTL) mints download tokens; endpoint rebuilds the ICS fresh each call so any future bug fix in `_build_ics` propagates without a token re-mint. **Admin now gets their own confirmation email** after clicking Accept (either from the email button OR the admin-panel button) — new `_build_admin_confirmation_html` renders the same 3 calendar buttons + prospect contact block; ICS is also attached for admin (mirrors prospect email exactly). Morning-of reminder emails on both sides now include the calendar buttons too, in case whoever missed the initial email still needs the event on their calendar. All calendar-anchoring uses `ZoneInfo("America/Los_Angeles")` for the Outlook UTC conversion so DST handoff (PDT ↔ PST) is honest — verified 10:00 AM PDT in July → 17:00 UTC in the Outlook URL. `_build_calendar_links` also handles the case where `scheduled_slot_index` was set (uses that as the source of truth) so a reschedule → re-accept cycle serves the right event without stale slot indexing. End-to-end verified with a synthetic row: `/demo/ics/<tok>` returned 200 + valid VCALENDAR w/ correct DTSTART / DTEND / VTIMEZONE.

- **2026-07-18 (Demo request — prospect-side cancel + reschedule with round-trip support):** Prospect confirmation email now carries two branded buttons: **📅 Change time** and **✗ Cancel demo**. Both are HMAC-signed with a new `URLSafeTimedSerializer(SECRET_KEY, salt="demo-cancel"|"demo-reschedule")` at 60-day TTL. **Cancel**: `GET /demo/cancel/{token}` shows a branded confirmation page (which slot is being released + a POST-only cancel button so a reflexive click doesn't nuke the demo). `POST /demo/cancel/{token}` moves status → `cancelled`, clears `scheduled_slot_index`/`scheduled_at`/`reminder_sent_at`, appends a `cancelled_by_prospect` entry to `history_json`, and fires two emails: red-gradient admin alert to `_get_admin_email(db)` + a short ack to the prospect. Idempotent — a second click renders "Already cancelled." **Reschedule**: `GET /api/demo-request/reschedule/{token}` returns prefill JSON (name/email/phone/entity/city/state/notes + `prior_slot_human`) and validates the token before the modal opens. Homepage `?reschedule=<token>` param on `demo-request-modal.js` (auto-opens on `DOMContentLoaded`, strips the token from the URL to avoid retriggering on reload, hits the GET, then opens the same modal in **reschedule mode** — swapped header/subhead/button label, prefilled fields with email + entity_type locked). Submit hits `POST /api/demo-request/reschedule/{token}` (rate-limited 6/hour) which replaces `preferred_slots_json`, **bumps `slots_version`**, moves status back → `pending`, nulls scheduled/reminder/responded timestamps, appends a `rescheduled_by_prospect` entry, then re-sends a fresh admin email with new accept buttons pinned to the new version. **Round-trip works across many rounds** — history keeps growing, `slots_version` keeps incrementing, and stale admin accept links from prior rounds get a "This request was rescheduled" branded page instead of accepting the wrong slot. Two additive DB columns on `demo_requests` — `history_json TEXT` (JSON array of `{ts, action, actor, prior_*, new_*}` entries) and `slots_version INTEGER DEFAULT 1` (encoded into accept token payload; verified server-side against the row on every accept-link click). Admin panel gets a new `Cancelled` status pill (red) + `Cancelled` filter option so cancelled rows stay visible in the queue. Verified end-to-end with a synthetic row: prefill GET → reschedule POST → status pending/v=2/slots swapped → cancel POST → status cancelled → repeat cancel → "Already cancelled" → v=1 accept token rejected with rescheduled-message page. `admin-demos.js?v=6`, `demo-request-modal.js?v=19`.

- **2026-07-16 (Demo request queue — homepage lead capture):** Custom "Request a Live Demo" flow. Homepage button on [index.html](app/index.html) opens a branded modal ([demo-request-modal.js](app/static/js/demo-request-modal.js)) collecting name/email/phone + entity_type/name + city/state + notes + **1-3 preferred time slots** (date + morning/afternoon/evening bucket in Pacific). Client submits to `POST /api/demo-request` — rate-limited 3/hour per IP, honeypot field for bot filter. New backend module [routes/demo_requests.py](backend/routes/demo_requests.py) manages the full lifecycle: schema `demo_requests` table in [db.py:2109](backend/db.py#L2109), tokens signed with `URLSafeTimedSerializer(SECRET_KEY, salt="demo-accept")` 30-day TTL. Admin gets a gradient-branded email immediately with three big **✓ Accept this slot** buttons — each a HMAC-signed magic link `GET /api/demo-request/{token}/accept` that admin can click straight from their phone with no login required. Accepting updates the DB, emails the prospect a branded confirmation with a **VCALENDAR (RFC 5545) attachment** including America/Los_Angeles VTIMEZONE, then renders a branded status page confirming what was booked. Admin panel adds a new **Demo Requests** tab in [admin.html](app/admin.html) with pending-count badge, filter (pending/all/scheduled/declined), and one-click accept/decline per card. Decline path opens an inline textarea for a custom message; prospect gets an email with the reason + prompt to reply with alternative times. Idempotent accept-link behavior: clicking a second slot on an already-scheduled request shows "already scheduled" instead of double-booking. Verified end-to-end with a synthetic prospect: submit returned 200, DB row landed with correct JSON slots, token roundtrip verified, ICS generation clean (837 bytes, valid VCALENDAR structure). 450 total routes, imports clean, API restarted successfully.

- **2026-07-13 (Charge failure = between venue and artist, admin off the loop):** Removed the three `_send_admin_alert` calls fired inside the venue-charge failure path at [payout_scheduler.py:265-320](backend/payout_scheduler.py#L265-L320): "Venue Card Missing", "Card Declined", and generic "Charge Error". Admin was previously emailed on every retry of every declined charge — noisy, and per owner: it's a venue/artist matter, not an admin one. The venue is already notified on **every** retry via `_send_venue_payment_warning` and on final failure via `_send_venue_suspended_email`; the artist is notified on final failure via `_send_charge_failed_email` (email) + `contract_charge_failed` in-app notification. `logger.error` breadcrumbs are unchanged inside `_handle_charge_failure` so admin can grep journalctl for forensics without inbox noise. Admin STILL gets alerted on genuine infrastructure issues that are theirs to fix: `No Stripe Key` at line 252, `CRITICAL: Stripe Package Not Installed` at 1009, `Payout Scheduler Error` at 1020, `Transfer Failed — Artist NOT Paid` at 1153. Scheduler restarted, bounce sweep clean.

- **2026-07-13 (Pre-launch hardening: unsubscribe RFC 8058, UFW, nginx rate limits):** Three infrastructure items I'd previously deferred as needing owner input. **RFC 8058 one-click unsubscribe (E-H2):** new [`backend/routes/unsubscribe.py`](backend/routes/unsubscribe.py) module with `POST /api/unsubscribe/{token}` (Gmail/Yahoo one-click) and `GET /api/unsubscribe/{token}` (visible footer-link fallback that renders a confirmation page). Tokens are `URLSafeTimedSerializer(SECRET_KEY, salt="email-unsub")` HMAC-signed `{uid, nt}` payloads with 90-day TTL — verifiable server-side without a DB lookup, unforgeable without SECRET_KEY. `email_service.send_notification_email` at [`email_service.py:324`](backend/email_service.py#L324) now mints one token per (user, notification_type) at send time and (1) sets `List-Unsubscribe: <https://.../api/unsubscribe/TOKEN>, <mailto:...>` + `List-Unsubscribe-Post: List-Unsubscribe=One-Click` on the message, (2) injects a visible "Unsubscribe from this type · Manage all preferences" footer into the body (idempotent — skips if body already carries `/api/unsubscribe/`). Endpoint calls the same `email_preferences.enabled=0` UPSERT `user_has_email_enabled` reads. Verified end-to-end: mint → GET (200 HTML) → POST (200 JSON) → DB row flipped → subsequent send skips. This closes the Feb-2024 Gmail/Yahoo bulk-sender folding-to-Promotions/Spam risk. **UFW (O-H2):** enabled with `default deny incoming`; explicit `allow 22/tcp, 80/tcp, 443/tcp` for both IPv4 and IPv6. All were confirmed as the only publicly-listening ports before enabling (uvicorn 8001 already 127.0.0.1-only; systemd-resolve 53 loopback-only). Site remained reachable throughout — external `curl https://gigsfill.com/` returned 200. **Nginx rate limiting (O-H3):** new [`/etc/nginx/conf.d/gigsfill-ratelimit.conf`](/etc/nginx/conf.d/gigsfill-ratelimit.conf) declares two zones — `gf_auth` (5r/s, 10m memory) for `/api/(login|signup|forgot-password|reset-password|resend-verification-email|change-password)` and `gf_api` (20r/s, 10m memory) for `/api/(gigs/public|venues/public|artists/public|cities|search)`. Two new `location ~ ^...` blocks in the site config apply the zones with `burst=10 nodelay` (auth) and `burst=40 nodelay` (api), returning HTTP 429 instead of 503 so clients + monitoring can tell rate-limit from outage. Zones are keyed on `$binary_remote_addr`. Pre-edit config backed up to `gigsfill.bak.pre-ratelimit`. Verified with burst tests: 50 rapid `/api/gigs/public` calls saw 30x 200 then 20x 429; 20 rapid empty POSTs to `/api/login` saw 12x 422 then 429s (validation still fires before rate limit, but the excess-request 429 hits the rest). Single normal traffic through the browser still 200s.

- **2026-07-13 (Post-audit sweep: remaining CRITICAL/HIGH items — email XSS, booking correctness, affiliate race, performance, ops):** Cleared the rest of the audit punch list beyond the 4 Stripe items. **E-C1 hold-email HTML escaping** ([gig_hold.py:1645](backend/services/gig_hold.py#L1645)): `_render()` now HTML-escapes every `{{var}}` substitution by default. Keys ending in `_html`/`_url`/`_href` skip escaping (opt-out for pre-composed markup). Closes real stored-XSS: an artist named `<img src=x onerror=fetch('//evil?c='+cookie)>` used to render live in every venue's inbox on hold accept/decline. **E-H1 email_dispatch XSS** ([email_dispatch.py:249, 414](backend/services/email_dispatch.py#L249)): escaped venue_address (both visible text AND href attribute) and artist_name/city/state fragments before interpolating into "safe" HTML blocks. **E-H6 hold-offer expiry TZ** ([gig_hold.py:1200](backend/services/gig_hold.py#L1200)): render in venue local time via `get_venue_timezone_str`, not raw UTC ISO. **E-H4 silent-pass on preferred-artist email errors** ([preferred_artists.py](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py)): 3 `except Exception as e: pass` blocks replaced with `logger.warning(f"...{e}")` so SMTP failures leave a paper trail. **E-H5 rate limits on approve/deny/revoke** ([preferred_artists.py](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py)): `@limiter.limit("10/minute")` on all three PUT endpoints + added `request: Request` param. Was: a malicious venue user could toggle status in a burst, firing full email fan-out per toggle. **B-C2 pending_approval_tokens expiry** ([db.py:2670](backend/db.py#L2670), [gigs.py:849, 5450, 5803](backend/routes/gigs.py#L849), [email_dispatch.py:1145](backend/services/email_dispatch.py#L1145)): added `expires_at` column with 72h default at insert; approve/deny handlers reject expired rows. Prevents replayable tokens from sitting valid forever if the venue never acts. **B-H1 cancel-blast day-window** ([gigs.py:6628](backend/routes/gigs.py#L6628)): use `get_venue_timezone_str(db, venue_id)` instead of platform TZ so a Hawaii venue near midnight lands in the correct owning window (36h/1w/2w/4w). **B-H2 recurring-update overlap** ([gigs.py:3958](backend/routes/gigs.py#L3958)): bulk `UPDATE gigs SET start_time/end_time` was skipping the same per-date overlap check `create_gig`/`update_gig` enforce. Now pre-flight overlap query with 409 listing conflicting dates. **B-H3 countersign ban recheck** ([contracts.py:1588](backend/routes/contracts.py#L1588)): before flipping the slot to `booked` + firing venue charge on countersign, re-check `venue_artist_bans` — a ban added between artist-sign (T) and countersign (T+48h) used to silently move money to a banned artist. Now 409 with clear message. **M-H4 affiliate quarterly race** ([affiliate.py:1089, 1198](backend/routes/affiliate.py#L1089)): SUM query now also captures `MAX(id) as snapshot_max_id`; the payout-link UPDATE scopes to `id <= snapshot_max_id` so any accrual between SUM and UPDATE stays `payout_id IS NULL` and rolls into next quarter. Was: affiliate underpaid AND those post-SUM rows marked "paid" forever. **P-C3 digest N+1 removed** ([open_gig_digest.py:359, 597](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py#L359)): added `COALESCE(g.frequency_exempt, 0) as frequency_exempt` to the outer SELECT and removed the per-gig SELECT. Was O(gigs × modes × artists × users) per digest sweep. **P-H7 get_notifications batch** ([notifications.py:75-125](backend/routes/notifications.py#L75)): parse all `(gig_id, slot_num)` pairs first, batch-fetch every referenced slot in one `WHERE gig_id IN (...)` query, then attach. Was up to 50 SELECTs per Activity Center open. **P-H8 hoist get_venue_timezone** ([preferred_artists.py:473](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py#L473)): venue TZ + today computation moved OUT of the per-artist loop — was O(N artists) get_venue_timezone calls all against the SAME venue. **D-M2 onboarding_visits schema** ([db.py:2094](backend/db.py#L2094)): `CREATE TABLE onboarding_visits` was missing from `setup_database()` — prod picked it up ambiently, fresh installs got 500s. Added canonical table + index. **O-H5 stale prod DB** — moved `/opt/gigsfill/backend_BU_3-14-26.db` (28M, mode 644 with live user data as of March) to `/backups/archive/backend_BU_3-14-26.db` (root:root, 600). **F-L11 cargo-cult logout** ([user-profile.js:1189](app/static/js/user-profile.js#L1189)): `document.cookie = "session_token="` did nothing because the cookie is HttpOnly; real logout must POST /api/logout. Fixed. **F-L10 dead HTML pages** — moved `modal-preview.html`, `diagnostics.html`, `index_Placeholder.html`, `index-comingsoon.html`, `index-app.html` out of `app/` (grep confirmed zero references). Backups in the session scratchpad in case of reversion. **F-M6 modals.js legacy show accepts raw HTML** ([modals.js:1](app/static/js/modals.js#L1)): `showModal()` now HTML-escapes `title` + `message` by default; added `showModalHTML()` opt-out for the one legitimate caller (`index-init.js:152` forgot-password modal HTML template). All import checks clean; API + scheduler restarted, active, zero exceptions in journalctl; scheduler bounce sweep ran clean; smoke tests pass. Full-site audit closed on all items I judged safe to auto-apply.

- **2026-07-13 (Post-audit: 4 money-critical Stripe fixes applied — M-C1, M-H1, M-H2, M-H3):** Fixed the four highest-priority findings from the overnight audit report. **M-C1 retry-transfer parent re-check** ([payout_scheduler.py:520-590](backend/payout_scheduler.py#L520)): before `stripe.Transfer.create` in the stalled-transfer sweep, re-read both parent (venue_charge) and child (artist_payout) status from DB. Between the outer SELECT and the Transfer.create, a `charge.refunded`/`dispute_lost` webhook may have flipped the parent → `payment_cancelled`; firing the transfer at that point moves funds to the artist even though the platform just refunded the venue. Now: if parent is not in `('charged','paid','transferred')` OR child is not in `('pending_transfer','transfer_failed','scheduled')`, skip the transfer, mark the child `payment_cancelled` with an auto-cancel note, fire an admin alert, and continue. Legacy 'single' rows without a parent get a PI-still-exists check. **M-H1 Refund.create idempotency** ([stripe_connect.py:1491-1510](backend/routes/stripe_connect.py#L1491)): added `idempotency_key=f"reinstate_slot_{slot_id}_fee_refund_{fee_to_refund}"`. Was the only Stripe write in the file without a key — double-clicked "Reinstate" or a network retry could refund the cancel fee twice. Slot+amount-scoped so a re-cancel→reinstate cycle at a different fee amount still gets its own key. **M-H2 venue-charge idempotency with attempt counter** ([payout_scheduler.py:286](backend/payout_scheduler.py#L286)): key was `gig_{gig_id}_txn_{txn_id}_charge` (no attempt suffix); when admin clicked Refire within 24h, Stripe replayed the cached CardError and the card was NOT re-charged despite the "refired" UI feedback. Now `gig_{gig_id}_txn_{txn_id}_charge_attempt_{attempts+1}` — matches the docstring at admin_payments.py:1062-1065. **M-H3 webhook dedup bypass** ([stripe_connect.py:2749-2767](backend/routes/stripe_connect.py#L2749)): the previous `except` on the atomic `INSERT OR IGNORE` set `_webhook_dedup_skip=True` and processed the event without the guard, with a comment saying "better to double-apply once than drop." That meant any transient sqlite lock during a Stripe redelivery re-ran full side effects (re-suspend, re-refund, re-clawback, re-alert). Now: raise `HTTPException(503)` so Stripe retries under exponential backoff (3-day window) with the dedup guard intact. Zero-drop, zero-duplicate. All four fixes verified: import check clean, API + scheduler restarted, no exceptions in journalctl, scheduler bounce sweep ran clean (0 errors).

- **2026-07-13 (Full-site pre-launch audit — 8 parallel dimensions, ~30 fixes applied, 4 CRITICAL still open):** Comprehensive overnight audit covering security, money, booking correctness, data integrity, frontend, ops, email, and performance. Full report at [/opt/gigsfill/AUDIT_REPORT_2026-07-13.md](AUDIT_REPORT_2026-07-13.md). **Applied fixes:** 6 stored-XSS holes (user-profile.js artist+venue name, venue.discovery.js venue name, venue.create-gigs.js cancel modal artist list, admin-init.js city/state, support-ticket-init.js, modals.js); media.py mass-assignment allowlist ([routes/media.py](backend/routes/media.py) — critical: previously let a caller `PUT /api/media/{id}` with `{"file_path": ".env"}` then `DELETE` to nuke server files); session cookie name fix in delete-account fallback ([me.py:721](backend/routes/me.py#L721) `"session"` → `"session_token"`); `book_with_contract` venue TZ regression ([contracts.py:2559](backend/routes/contracts.py#L2559) — was missing `venue_id=` on `_is_same_day_booking`, undoing the May 2026 Hawaii/Alaska fix); `PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON` added to every raw `sqlite3.connect()` in [db.py](backend/db.py) (was silent-off, letting orphan rows through — that's how the venue=3/user=6 orphans got in); 5 missing indexes seeded in `setup_database()` (`transactions.parent_transaction_id`, `.stripe_payment_intent_id`, `payment_methods.user_id`, `venue_reviews(venue_id, is_visible)`, `gig_email_log(notification_key, gig_id)`) plus the partial UNIQUE `idx_gig_slots_hold` that closes the double-booking race CLAUDE.md warned about but wasn't enforced; dropped duplicate `idx_transactions_gig_artist_unique`; normalized 47 rows in `notifications.created_at` with `+00:00` suffix so `ORDER BY created_at DESC` returns the right order; cleaned 2 orphan FK-violating rows (`venues.id=3`, `entity_users(venue,3,user=6)` — dangling from a hard-delete of `users.id=6`); `chmod 640 /opt/gigsfill/.env` (was world-readable with live Stripe secret + TIN encryption key — real ~6-week exposure window; **RECOMMEND KEY ROTATION**); `chown www-data:www-data /opt/gigsfill/backend/email_templates.py` (was root-owned, silently breaking admin auto-export per CLAUDE.md). All import checks clean, API + scheduler restarted, 443 routes load, curl smoke tests pass. **Still open (see report for detail):** M-C1 retry-transfer parent-status re-check (payout_scheduler.py) → money loss on refunded parent; M-H1 Refund.create missing idempotency key (stripe_connect.py:1491); M-H2 venue-charge idempotency omits attempt counter (payout_scheduler.py:286); M-H3 webhook dedup bypass on DB hiccup (stripe_connect.py:2740-2745); M-H4 affiliate quarterly UPDATE race; B-C2 `pending_approval_tokens` no expiry (replayable forever); E-C1 hold-email pipeline bypasses HTML escaping (gig_hold.py:1659); 3 CRITICAL unbounded query paths (list_gigs, list_venue_gigs, digest per-row lookup). Deferred deliberately: these are either careful design changes (transfer re-check, idempotency scheme) or infrastructure (UFW enable, droplet resize) that shouldn't be applied at 4 AM without owner review. Full prioritized punch list + reasoning in the audit report file.

- **2026-07-13 (Deep-dive delete-endpoint audit — 30+ endpoints reviewed, all critical/high findings fixed):** Full sweep of every `@router.delete` / `@app.delete` route + every raw `DELETE FROM` in service code before real-world launch. Findings and fixes:

  **Auth bypasses closed:**
  - [`main.py:1292` DELETE /api/venues/{v}/invitations/{i}](backend/main.py#L1292) had **zero** authorization check — any authenticated user could cancel any venue's pending artist invitations. Added `check_venue_access`.
  - [`venue_emails.py:307`](backend/routes/venue_emails.py#L307) `delete_venue_email_history_item` called `check_venue_access(row["venue_id"], user.id, db)` — args in wrong order AND treated the return as a bool. Endpoint was either 500 or 403-for-everyone. Fixed the call.
  - [`entity_users.py:_remove_user_from_entity`](backend/routes/entity_users.py) accepted **any** entity_users row as authorization — a low-tier team member could kick out other team members. Now requires role in {owner, admin, manager} OR original owner.
  - [`stripe_connect.py:223`](backend/routes/stripe_connect.py#L223) `remove_venue_payment_method` was owner-only (`venue["user_id"] != user.id`). Now uses `check_venue_access` so entity_users co-managers can rotate the card without escalation.

  **Data-loss / cascade completeness:**
  - [`admin.py:_PROTECTED_TABLES`](backend/routes/admin.py) — the admin DB inspector's protected-table set was missing `artists`, `venues`, `venue_contracts`, `artist_reviews`, `venue_reviews`, `notifications`, `preferred_artists`, `venue_artist_bans`, `connect_account_health`. Raw DELETE via the tool would bypass the tombstone pipeline (no in-flight cancel, no notifications, no file cleanup, orphaned FKs). All added to the block list.
  - [`reviews.py:675`](backend/routes/reviews.py) `delete_venue_review` — DUPLICATE route registration with `delete_my_general_venue_review` at line 547. FastAPI kept first-registered; the second (which would nuke gig-specific reviews too) was dead code. Deleted the dead handler.
  - [`services/entity_delete.py:delete_artist`](backend/services/entity_delete.py) was silently orphaning: `connect_account_health` (kept a Stripe account handle → scheduler polled a ghost forever), `artist_email_digest_queue`, `artist_availability`, `venue_artist_bans` (this artist's row on every venue's ban list), artist-owned `flyers`, `artist_gig_notes`, `review_link_tokens`. All added to the cascade with per-statement `try/except` + `logger.info` on failure.
  - [`services/entity_delete.py:delete_venue`](backend/services/entity_delete.py) parallel additions: `artist_email_digest_queue WHERE venue_id`, `venue_artist_bans WHERE venue_id`, `review_link_tokens WHERE venue_id`.
  - [`routes/gigs.py:delete_gig_with_slots` (delete branch)](backend/routes/gigs.py) — the venue UI's primary "Cancel Gig" target — reimplemented cleanup inline and drifted from the canonical helper. Missed `gig_cancelled_artists`, `notification_sent_log`, `venue_reviews.gig_id` NULL-out, recurring-series survivor cleanup, `_recompute_gig_fees`, empty signed-contract-directory removal. Replaced the whole inline block with a call to `delete_gig_completely(db, gig_id)` after cleaning the four gig-scoped extras that helper doesn't cover (email log, public_activity, gig-scoped flyers, payment_cancellations).
  - Legacy [`DELETE /gigs/{id}`](backend/routes/gigs.py) at line 3419 — still routed, still reachable, but notification path was `if result["status"] == "booked" and result["artist_id"]` which silently skipped `pending_contract`/`awaiting_venue_contract`/`pending_venue_approval` states AND only notified the parent `gigs.artist_id` (missing all multi-slot booked artists). Now enumerates every in-flight `gig_slots` row and notifies each artist.

  **Correctness:**
  - [`routes/gigs.py:cancel_slot`](backend/routes/gigs.py) — `last_cancelled_artist_id = :aid` binding used `slot.get("artist_id") or 0` fallback. When a slot with no artist got cancelled (remove_slot on an already-open slot), the gig ended up with `last_cancelled_artist_id = 0`. Both UPDATE statements changed to pass `None` explicitly and wrap the write in `CASE WHEN :aid IS NOT NULL THEN :aid ELSE last_cancelled_artist_id END` so the field only ever holds a real artist id.

  **File cleanup ordering (commit-safety):**
  - [`services/entity_delete.py:delete_artist` and `delete_venue`](backend/services/entity_delete.py) — previously ran `_rm_tree` synchronously BEFORE `db.commit()` in the route wrappers. A commit-fail would restore DB state but the media/flyers/contract PDFs were already gone → live entity with no assets. Refactored: both helpers now RETURN a list of dir paths, and the three callers ([`venues.py:928`](backend/routes/venues.py#L928), [`artists.py:726`](backend/routes/artists.py#L726), [`me.py:493`](backend/routes/me.py#L493)) call `_rm_tree` on that list AFTER a successful commit.
  - [`routes/contracts.py:669`](backend/routes/contracts.py#L669) `delete_venue_contract` — deleted the DB row but never removed the uploaded PDF under `app/static/uploads/contracts/venue_{v}/contract_{c}_*.pdf` (unbounded disk growth). Now snapshots `pdf_file_path` before the DB delete and unlinks after commit.

  **Audit logging:**
  - All three cancel-a-gig endpoints ([`cancel_gig`, `cancel_slot`, `delete_gig_with_slots`](backend/routes/gigs.py)) now call `log_admin_action` with the relevant metadata (gig_id / slot_id / keep_open / cancellation_reason / booked artist ids). Money-adjacent state changes (transactions, contracts, flyers) had ZERO audit trail before.
  - `entity_delete.delete_artist` and `delete_venue` write `entity_tombstone_artist` / `entity_tombstone_venue` actions to admin_audit_log via a small `_ActorShim` (the helpers only receive `user_id`, so we synthesize the actor).
  - [`notifications.py:160`](backend/routes/notifications.py#L160) `delete_notification` — previously returned silent `ok:True` even when no row matched (ambiguity between "already gone" and "wrong id / not yours"). Now returns 404 on rowcount=0.

  **Smoke tests passed:** all 443 routes load; `curl` against public endpoints returns 200; unauthenticated DELETE calls against the previously-open venue invitation route now correctly return 401 instead of quietly proceeding. Both API and scheduler services restarted clean.

  **Deferred (not blockers):**
  - Race protection on standalone entity deletes (rate limit / SELECT FOR UPDATE) — audited but declined; SQLite serializes writes and pre-launch data cleanup will happen at go-live.
  - `_LIVE_TXN_STATUSES` completeness sweep vs `payment_scheduler.py` — flagged for a payment-specific audit pass, not touched here.
  - Postgres compat (`DATE('now', 'localtime')` is SQLite-only) — production runs SQLite; flagged for the Postgres-migration sprint.
  - Generic `DELETE /api/media/{id}` id-collision between ArtistMedia and VenueMedia — flagged; deprecate in favor of type-specific endpoints.
  - `media.py` `lstrip("/")` relative-path — works in prod because systemd sets `WorkingDirectory=/opt/gigsfill`; would break if uvicorn ran from a different cwd. Defense-in-depth cleanup for later.

- **2026-07-13 (`preferred_artists` audit trail):** Incident investigation — Fifty Proof's row at 14 Cannons vanished between May 10 and Jul 13 with no reconstructable cause. Ruled out every audited code path (venue not tombstoned, artist not tombstoned, no ban record). Also discovered venue IDs 5–8 are hard-deleted from the `venues` table (audited delete only tombstones — those were raw SQL from an earlier maintenance session outside the app). Row restored manually with `INSERT INTO preferred_artists ...` — Fifty Proof preferred at 14 Cannons with `pay_dollars_override=15`. Defense-in-depth: added `log_admin_action` calls to **every** `preferred_artists` mutation endpoint so the same class of loss leaves a paper trail next time — [approve](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py) (action `preferred_approve`), [deny](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py) (`preferred_deny`), [revoke](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py) (`preferred_revoke`), [override update](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py) (`preferred_override_update`, captures before/after JSON), [ban-and-delete](backend/routes/preferred_artists.py) (`preferred_delete_by_ban`, snapshots the row into `before_json` so a forensic reconstruction is possible even after the row is gone), and [venues.py proactive approve](backend/routes/venues.py) (`preferred_proactive_approve`). Also added `logger.info` breadcrumbs in [entity_delete.py](backend/services/entity_delete.py) around the two `DELETE FROM preferred_artists WHERE (artist_id|venue_id)` calls, logging the affected row count before the wipe — that way journalctl carries a trace even for the automated cascade paths. Verified end-to-end: `log_admin_action` writes land in `admin_audit_log`, module imports clean, API restarted successfully. Pre-launch data cleanup will happen at go-live; the audit-log wiring is the durable protection going forward.

- **2026-07-12 (Digest follow-ups: variants sorted highest-pay first, booked-section variants, admin trigger uses live pathway):** Three targeted follow-ups on top of the morning's variant work. (1) `_finalize_variant_gigs` now sorts each slot's `variants[]` by `effective_pay DESC` so the top-paying artist for a slot renders first — the eye lands on the best opportunity. Stable sort preserves artist-id order on ties. (2) Extended the variant treatment to the booked-gigs section — previously a multi-slot gig where Fridays Past held Slot 1 and Fifty Proof held Slot 2 silently dropped one artist via the dedup-by-gig-id branch. Now both slots render with their holding artist tagged inline (e.g. "Slot 1 · time · **Fridays Past** $10 · Booked"). The `not is_booked_section` guard in `_fmt_line` has been removed so the variant path applies uniformly. (3) Refactored the per-user digest build into a new module-level helper `build_digest_for_user(cursor, user_id) -> dict` at [open_gig_digest.py](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py) that both `send_daily_artist_digest` (scheduler tick) and `admin_force_send_digest` at [admin.py:3039](backend/routes/admin.py#L3039) call — closing the divergence where the admin "force send" endpoint used the legacy queue-based renderer while real users got the live version. Tested end-to-end: sent a real digest to user_id=1 via `build_digest_for_user`, 5 open gigs at 1 venue (14 Cannons), scheduler + API both restarted clean. The `[ADMIN-RESEND]` and `[RESENT]` endpoints in [admin.py:2950](backend/routes/admin.py#L2950) and [emails.py:341](backend/routes/emails.py#L341) still use the legacy renderer deliberately — those are historical replays of what was actually sent at a specific past minute, not previews of current content.

- **2026-07-12 (Digest: per-slot per-artist variants for multi-artist users):** A user with multiple artists was getting one merged digest, but the send loop dedup-by-gig-id at [open_gig_digest.py](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py) silently dropped the second artist's variant of any shared gig — so a gig with Fridays Past base pay $10 and Fifty Proof override pay $15 emailed as "$10" (whichever artist had the lower id ran first). Real money left on the table + confusing when the user logged in as the other artist and saw a different rate. Fixed: dedup replaced with variant-aware aggregation via new helpers `_merge_gig_variants()` + `_finalize_variant_gigs()`. Each gig row now carries per-slot `variants: [{artist_name, effective_pay}]`. Renderer expands multi-variant slots to `Slot N · time · Fridays Past $10.00 · Fifty Proof $15.00` (each artist listed with their own pay), or `Slot N · time · Fifty Proof $15.00` when only one of the user's artists qualifies for that slot. `_render_digest_email_live()` takes a new `multi_artist_user` flag — single-artist users see the unchanged one-pay-per-slot format. Applies to open-gigs section and nearby-non-preferred section; booked section left alone (bookings are already artist-specific). Verified end-to-end against a real multi-artist user (id=1) and a synthetic gig with per-artist override.

- **2026-07-10 (Daily open-gig digest send time: 9 AM → 6 AM local):** Changed the artist daily-digest send hour from 9 to 6 in each artist's local time zone — request was to hit inboxes before the workday, not mid-morning. Semantics unchanged (still fires at the artist's LOCAL hour matching `platform_settings.open_gig_daily_digest_hour`, not a single platform-wide Pacific send). Updated the seeded default in [db.py:1553](backend/db.py#L1553) `"9" → "6"`, the missing-row fallback in [open_gig_digest.py:1329](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py#L1329), the admin summary fallback in [admin.py:2894](backend/routes/admin.py#L2894), and the docstring in [utils.py:204](backend/utils.py#L204). Also ran `UPDATE platform_settings SET setting_value='6' WHERE setting_key='open_gig_daily_digest_hour'` on the production SQLite DB so the change takes effect immediately (the code-side default only kicks in for fresh installs; existing installs keep whatever's in the DB). No scheduler restart needed — `send_daily_artist_digest` reads the setting fresh on every tick.

- **2026-07-06 (Single-slot → multi-slot unification, Phase 1 & Phase 2 shipped):** CLAUDE.md flagged that "Single-slot vs multi-slot gigs branch in ~70 places." Audit confirmed: 77 direct `is_multi_slot` refs, ~50 dual-read fallbacks, 5 UNION queries, 10+ frontend files. Rather than a 2-3-day full-rip refactor (which touches the payment path — highest money-risk in the codebase), this session ships the two safest phases that unlock most of the visual/UX unification.
  1. **Phase 1 — Invariant established** ([db.py:setup_database](backend/db.py)). Backfilled 4 legacy single-slot gigs (550, 551, 552, 555) with synthesized `gig_slots` rows copied from gig-level fields (start_time / end_time / pay / artist_id / artist_type / band_formats / styles → new slot row with `slot_number=1, deal_type='flat'`). Normalized `is_multi_slot=1` across all 58 gigs. Added the same INSERT + UPDATE to `setup_database()` (idempotent — runs on every startup, no-op after first pass) so backup restores or externally-inserted legacy rows normalize automatically. **New invariant: every gig has ≥1 gig_slots row and `is_multi_slot=1`.** This unblocks every downstream reader to treat every gig as slot-shaped.
  2. **Phase 2a — Dead-code fallbacks retired.** With the invariant established, "legacy single-slot fallback" branches that synthesized slot data from gig-level umbrella fields are now unreachable. Removed:
     - [email_dispatch.py:309-320](backend/services/email_dispatch.py) `send_booking_emails` gig.artist_id fallback (synthesized a slot from gig-level pay/times when no booked_slots came back — now just logs and returns since that case can't happen)
     - [email_dispatch.py:876-889](backend/services/email_dispatch.py) `send_contract_sign_needed_emails` legacy single-slot slot_rows_html fallback
     - [open_gig_digest.py:557-565](backend/services/open_gig_digest.py) `_fetch_artist_open_gigs_live` `else`-branch for slotless gigs (also guarded upstream by the outer SQL's `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM gig_slots ...)`)
  3. **Phase 2b — Contract countersign `is_multi_slot` branch collapsed** ([contracts.py:1744-1786](backend/routes/contracts.py)). Was: if multi-slot, DON'T write gigs.artist_id (to avoid leaking a stale artist to public API); if single-slot, DO write it. Now: **NEVER write gigs.artist_id.** The booked artist lives on `gig_slots.artist_id`. This eliminates the leak the branch was working around (May 2026 incident) and simplifies to one code path.
  4. **Phase 2c — Calendar feed UNION leg dropped** ([calendar_feed.py:268-334](backend/routes/calendar_feed.py)). The .ics feed's SELECT was a UNION of (single-slot gigs read from gigs.artist_id/pay/times) + (multi-slot gigs read from gig_slots). Post-backfill, both legs return the same rows for every gig — the single-slot leg was dupe data. Dropped the leg entirely; feed now purely reads from `gig_slots`.

  **Deliberately deferred** (belt-and-suspenders is safer than a half-rip):
  - `update_gig` still writes gig-level pay/times/type/formats/styles as dupes of slot data — the audit found ~30 reader sites still use them as the umbrella when displaying gigs. Freezing writes without simultaneously rewriting readers would leave stale gig-level fields visible in emails. Deferred to a separate sprint.
  - `_create_booking_transaction`, `book_gig`, `book_slot`, and the whole payment path still branch — highest money-risk zone in the codebase, not touching pre-launch.
  - Payout scheduler still uses slot-first-gig-fallback pay reads — dead-code post-backfill but harmless.
  - Frontend `data.is_multi_slot` reads still work correctly (all gigs are `is_multi_slot=1` now) so no visual change needed.
  - Column drops (`gigs.artist_id / start_time / end_time / pay / artist_type / band_formats / styles`) — need every reader gone first.

  **What the venue sees now**: same UX as before, but the 4 legacy slotless gigs (550, 551, 552, 555) now render with the same "Slot 1 · time · pay" pattern as every other gig — verified via digest for Fridays Past: nearby-section rendered 3 of them with slot data (previously they were invisible in the digest because the SQL required an `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM gig_slots ...)`). All 4 test endpoints returned 200. Zero errors in logs. Services restarted clean.

  **Phase 2b — More UNIONs collapsed + real bug found in analytics:**
  1. **`scheduler.py:376-405`** — Day-of gig-confirmation email query. Was UNION of (single-slot: `WHERE g.artist_id IS NOT NULL`) + (multi-slot: JOIN gig_slots). Dropped the single-slot leg — post-backfill it's a duplicate.
  2. **`analytics.py:top_venues_booked` + `top_artists_booked`** — Was UNION ALL of `SELECT venue_id/artist_id FROM gigs WHERE artist_id IS NOT NULL` + `SELECT ... FROM gig_slots ... WHERE status='booked'`. **Both legs returned the same booking for single-slot gigs → DOUBLE-COUNT.** Verified: 20 real booked slots but analytics UNION was returning 26. Fixed to slot-only. Top-artists list now shows correct counts (Fridays Past: 15×, Fifty Proof: 5×).
  3. **`analytics.py:recent_bookings`** — Same double-count pattern in the recent-bookings list. Dropped single-slot leg. Now shows one row per booked slot, ordered by slot creation time.
  4. **`availability.py:booked_conflicts`** — Booking-conflict detection when an artist tries to add a blackout. Was `WHERE g.artist_id = :aid OR EXISTS (gig_slots ...)`. Dropped the `g.artist_id` OR-leg — slot-only.

  **Impact of the analytics fix** (real regression flushed out during refactor): the admin dashboard's "top artists" and "top venues" lists were reporting 30% inflated booking counts for anyone with single-slot bookings. Fridays Past was showing 21 bookings but only had 15 real ones. This bug pre-existed the Jul 2026 backfill (single-slot writes populated both `gigs.artist_id` AND `gig_slots.artist_id`) but became more obvious when the backfill added slot rows for the 4 legacy gigs.

  **Still on the punchlist for a follow-up sprint** (safe to launch without):
  - `gigs.py` ~30 read sites still reference `g.artist_id` (some as `LEFT JOIN artists a ON g.artist_id = a.id` for display — harmless; some as `WHERE g.artist_id = :aid` — silently miss multi-slot bookings)
  - `me.py:408/514/529`, `messages.py:120`, `artists.py:373/619/622`, `flyers.py:272` — same pattern
  - `stripe_connect.py:2328/3401` upcoming payouts `OR g.artist_id = :aid` fallback
  - `admin_payments.py:361` same fallback pattern
  - `preferred_artists.py:342-408` — 5-leg UNION for "next gig date" in the My Artists list
  - Frontend `data.is_multi_slot` reads — all functionally correct post-backfill (every gig is `is_multi_slot=1`) so no bug, just dead branches waiting to be swept

  **Sprint 2 estimate**: ~1 day of careful `grep → replace → test` work to sweep the remaining ~30 sites. Nothing on this list is blocking launch; all currently produce correct output (or over-count safely). Do this AFTER launch when you have live traffic to validate against.

  **Phase 2c — Sprint-2 punchlist mostly cleared same day (11 additional sites):**
  1. **`preferred_artists.py:337-416`** — the 5-leg UNION monster (venue "My Artists" list "next gig date" + tiebreaker) collapsed to slot-only. Was three separate correlated subqueries, each doing a 2-way UNION of (single-slot: `gigs.artist_id`) + (slot: `gig_slots.artist_id`), and the tiebreaker subquery nested another 2-way UNION inside it. Now: three simple slot-only subqueries. Line count roughly halved.
  2. **`me.py:398/509`** — account-summary + delete-account cascade queries. Dropped `(g.artist_id = a.id OR gs.id IS NOT NULL)` LEFT JOIN pattern in favor of straight `JOIN gig_slots gs`.
  3. **`stripe_connect.py:2321/3400`** — upcoming charges + earnings-summary payload. Dropped `OR g.artist_id = :aid` belt-and-suspenders leg. `t.artist_id` is authoritative on transactions.
  4. **`admin_payments.py:361`** — same fallback pattern in the admin payments filter.
  5. **`artists.py:355-399`** — `/api/artists/{id}/venues` was running two separate queries (single-slot + slot) then merging with DISTINCT in Python. Post-backfill both return identical rows. Merged into one slot-only query; retired the second.
  6. **`messages.py:117-129`** — identity-resolution for conversation lookup had a fallback single-artist-row lookup via `g.artist_id`. Post-backfill the slot lookup above always finds the match. Fallback query converted to a no-op stub (`WHERE 0=1`) to preserve the downstream `if single_artist_row:` branch shape without further code changes.
  7. **`flyers.py:277-291`** — `if is_multi_slot:` branch that returned `slots = []` for single-slot flyers. Post-backfill all gigs are multi-slot, so the else branch was unreachable. Always fetch slots.
  8. **`gigs.py:1338 list_artist_gigs_public`** — the classic two-query merge pattern (single-slot via `gigs.artist_id` + slot via `gig_slots.artist_id`, then Python-side `seen_ids` dedup). Post-backfill both return identical rows. Collapsed to one slot-only query. Verified: Fridays Past returns 15 gigs (matches analytics count), Fifty Proof returns 5.
  9. **`gigs.py:1889 gig-conflict-check`** — the "does this artist have an overlapping booking?" query had both `g.artist_id = :aid AND status IN ...` and `gs.artist_id = :aid AND status IN ...` OR-legs. Collapsed to slot-only INNER JOIN.
  10. **`gigs.py:2185 + 4898` frequency-check** — the "closest recent booking at this venue" query used `AND (g.artist_id=:aid OR EXISTS(gig_slots))` pattern in two places (single-slot + multi-slot booking paths). Both collapsed to slot-only JOIN.

  **Effect on real queries**:
